Product problem：enterprise users want AI to complete tasks, not just answer。 · AI/data/model challenge：tool selection、memory、RAG、permission-aware planning。 · Systems architecture：tool registry、policy engine、durable workflow、audit/replay。 · Impact：safer automation、higher task completion、lower support workload。

8. Knowledge Map：你要展示的 depth + width#

Product depth：

user workflow。 · success metrics。 · failure states。 · customer trust。 · enterprise governance。

Model depth：

model lifecycle。 · eval gates。 · prompt/template versioning。 · embedding/reranker/version。 · model routing。

Data depth：

data lineage。 · dataset manifest。 · vector index。 · ACL/delete/freshness。 · quality validation。

System depth：

control plane/data plane。 · workflow engine。 · distributed scheduling。 · cache。 · observability。 · deployment/rollback。

Ops depth：

runbook。 · incident。 · SLO。 · cost attribution。 · on-call。 · SOC2 evidence。

Algorithm depth：

top-k merge。 · HNSW/IVF/PQ。 · token bucket。 · priority queue。 · MMR。 · nDCG/MRR。 · state machine。 · consistent hashing。

9. Product Metrics Practice：让别人知道你关心 product#

不要只说 system metrics。你要主动补 product metrics。

Creative AI：

time-to-preview。 · accepted generation rate。 · edit iteration count。 · export/share rate。 · cost per accepted asset。 · provenance coverage。

RAG / Document AI：

citation click rate。 · answer accepted。 · grounded answer rate。 · retrieval recall。 · faithfulness。 · user follow-up rate。

Agent：

task completion rate。 · tool success rate。 · human escalation rate。 · policy violation rate。 · rollback success。 · cost per completed task。

Forecasting：

forecast adoption。 · scenario creation。 · override/edit rate。 · export/share。 · backtest error。 · calibration。

Semantic Index：

query success。 · recall@k。 · filtered recall。 · index freshness lag。 · deletion propagation time。 · cost per million queries。

面试金句：

I like tying infra metrics to product metrics. Low latency is useful only if it improves time-to-preview, task completion, answer trust, or customer adoption.

10. Stack Practice：同一项目如何讲 stack 但不显得 tool-driven？#

弱回答：

We use Kafka, Kubernetes, vector DB, and Prometheus.

强回答：

The stack maps to lifecycle responsibilities. Kafka carries replayable workflow and telemetry events. Kubernetes runs scalable workers. Vector DB supports retrieval, but index lifecycle is controlled by our registry and rollout system. Prometheus/Grafana show low-cardinality SLO metrics, while detailed model/index events go to warehouse for product and cost analysis.

Semantic Index stack：

Kafka/PubSub：ingestion events。 · Spark/Ray/Flink：embedding/index build processing。 · S3/GCS：vector/index artifacts。 · Milvus/Qdrant/Weaviate/Pinecone/OpenSearch：serving layer。 · Postgres/DynamoDB：control plane metadata。 · Prometheus/Grafana/OTel：ops。 · Airflow/Argo/Temporal：lifecycle workflow。

Agent stack：

API gateway。 · LangGraph/custom planner。 · Temporal for durable workflow。 · tool registry。 · policy engine。 · vector/RAG memory。 · audit store。 · eval harness。

Creative AI stack：

object store。 · FFmpeg/GStreamer/OpenCV/libvips。 · workflow engine。 · vLLM/VLM/diffusion/video/audio models。 · asset graph DB。 · provenance service。 · CDN/export service。

11. Algorithm Practice：把算法和产品系统联系起来#

你不需要像 research scientist 一样推所有公式，但要能解释算法为什么影响 product/system。

Top-k merge：

用于 distributed vector search。 · 影响 latency 和 recall。 · 需要处理 shard timeout 和 partial result。

MMR：

用于 RAG context 去重和 creative search diversity。 · 影响 answer coverage 和 user-visible variety。

Token bucket：

用于 tenant quota 和 cost protection。 · 影响 fairness 和 abuse prevention。

Priority queue：

用于 GPU jobs / media rendering。 · 影响 time-to-preview 和 enterprise SLA。

State machine：

用于 workflow/job/model deployment。 · 影响 retry/resume/rollback。

Consistent hashing：

用于 cache/index/model shard routing。 · 影响 remapping and cache hit。

面试金句：

I try to explain algorithms in terms of the product or operational property they protect: latency, recall, fairness, safety, reproducibility, or cost.

12. Challenging Project：AI Product System Unification Layer#

这是一个你可以用来练的综合项目，把身份定位讲完整。

Problem：

公司有多个 AI features：RAG assistant、creative generation、semantic search、agent workflows、customer-specific models。每个团队都自己做 data handling、model routing、eval、deployment、logging，导致重复建设、质量不一致、回滚困难、customer trust 风险。

Project：

构建 AI Product System Unification Layer：

unified AI request context。 · model/data/index registry。 · workflow state。 · evaluation gates。 · customer/tenant context。 · deployment and rollback。 · observability schema。 · cost attribution。

Architecture：

text
Product Feature
  -> AI Request Context
  -> Policy / Tenant / Product Workflow
  -> Data + Model + Index Resolver
  -> Execution: LLM / RAG / Agent / Media / Forecast
  -> Artifact + Evaluation + Observability
  -> Deployment / Rollback / Audit

Key abstraction：

python
from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AIRequestContext:
    product: str
    tenant_id: str
    user_id_hash: str
    workflow_id: str
    model_version: str | None
    data_version: str | None
    index_version: str | None
    policy_version: str
    trace_id: str

Why it matters：

Product teams move faster。 · Model/data/index changes are traceable。 · Customer isolation is consistent。 · Deployment/rollback becomes safer。 · Observability and cost attribution become reusable。

Interview pitch：

This is the kind of platform work I like: not abstract infrastructure for its own sake, but a system contract that lets product teams build AI features faster and safer.

13. Q&A：如何回答 “你是 infra 还是 product？”#

Q：Are you more infra or product?

A：

I am an engineer with strong infrastructure depth, but I do not see infrastructure as the boundary of my role. I work best on AI product systems where product workflow, data, model behavior, serving, deployment, and operations all have to fit together.

Q：How deep are you in ML?

A：

I am not positioning myself as a model researcher, but I understand the ML systems layer deeply: embedding versioning, retrieval quality, eval, model lifecycle, inference constraints, data quality, and how model behavior affects product reliability.

Q：Have you worked beyond deployment?

A：

Yes. Deployment is only one part. I think about the full lifecycle: data ingestion, validation, model/index versioning, evaluation, rollout, monitoring, customer impact, rollback, and operations.

Q：Why AI infra if you do not want to be pure infra?

A：

Because AI infra is where product behavior becomes real. The infra decisions determine latency, quality, trust, cost, and safety. I am interested in AI systems where infrastructure is deeply connected to product and model outcomes.

Q：How do you work with ML researchers?

A：

I try to turn research outputs into production contracts: what model artifacts exist, what eval gates matter, what data/version assumptions are required, what latency/cost constraints exist, and how rollback works if the model regresses.

Q：How do you work with product/design?

A：

I map system behavior to user workflow: loading states, preview/final paths, failure states, human approval, review, export, and trust signals like citations or provenance.

13. Q&A：如何回答 “你不是 pure infra，那你能写底层系统吗？”#

回答：

Yes. My positioning is broader than infra, but I still go deep technically. For example, on a semantic index platform I can discuss sharding, HNSW/IVF/PQ tradeoffs, top-k merge, delete propagation, filtered recall, and index rollout. On LLM serving I can discuss KV cache, admission control, TTFT/TPOT, batching, and GPU memory. The difference is that I connect those details to product outcomes.

这句话很关键：

Breadth does not mean lack of depth. It means I know which deep technical problems matter for the product.

14. Resume Bullet Rewrites#

如果原 bullet 是：

Built infrastructure for AI model deployment.

改成：

Built production AI platform capabilities connecting model registry, evaluation gates, canary rollout, rollback, observability, and customer-scoped deployment.

如果原 bullet 是：

Worked on vector index service.

改成：

Built semantic retrieval infrastructure for RAG and AI search, including embedding/index versioning, shard lifecycle, ACL-aware retrieval, deletion propagation, recall evaluation, and latency/cost monitoring.

如果原 bullet 是：

Improved monitoring.

改成：

Designed AI product observability linking product workflow, model version, data/index version, infrastructure metrics, customer impact, and cost attribution.

如果原 bullet 是：

Built media pipeline.

改成：

Built creative AI workflow infrastructure for image/video/audio assets, including async jobs, asset lineage, model routing, preview/final rendering, provenance, and export readiness.

15. Behavioral Stories To Prepare#

Story 1：Ambiguous AI product requirement

Situation：product wanted AI feature but workflow unclear。 · Action：mapped user workflow, data/model needs, infra constraints。 · Result：defined platform contract and shipped safely。

Story 2：Model or index regression caught before launch

Situation：new model/index looked fine in service metrics but quality regressed。 · Action：added eval gate/canary/slice analysis。 · Result：prevented bad rollout。

Story 3：Cross-functional conflict

Situation：product wanted speed, security/ML/infra had concerns。 · Action：split preview/final, feature flag, policy gate, staged rollout。 · Result：fast iteration with guardrails。

Story 4：Operational maturity

Situation：incidents or manual ops repeated。 · Action：runbook, dashboard, owner, rollback, automation。 · Result：MTTR down, shipping confidence up。

Story 5：Cost/product tradeoff

Situation：GPU or model cost growing。 · Action：cost attribution, routing, quota, right-sizing。 · Result：cost down without hurting product metrics。

16. Practice Drills#

Drill A：用 60 秒讲你是谁。

必须包含：

AI product systems。 · not pure infra。 · model/data/deployment/ops。 · product outcome。

Drill B：用 2 分钟讲一个项目。

必须包含：

product problem。 · model/data challenge。 · architecture。 · algorithm。 · ops。 · impact。

Drill C：把一个 infra 词改成 product systems 词。

deployment -> release safety and product quality。 · monitoring -> product/model/infra observability。 · vector DB -> semantic retrieval foundation。 · GPU scheduler -> product capacity and cost control。 · pipeline -> lifecycle system。

Drill D：回答三个 objection。

“Are you infra?” · “How deep are you in ML?” · “What product metrics did you move?”

17. Final Positioning Cheat Sheet#

你希望面试官记住：

你能写系统和代码。 · 你懂 AI product workflow。 · 你懂 model/data lifecycle。 · 你懂 RAG/agent/vector/GPU/serving。 · 你懂 deployment/ops/security/customer trust。 · 你能跨团队推动。

最终版本：

I am a product-minded AI systems engineer. I go deep into infrastructure, but I use that depth to build reliable AI product systems: semantic retrieval, agent runtime, creative workflows, model lifecycle, deployment safety, observability, and customer trust.

AI Product Model Infra System Design Manual#

这部分是把 aiInfraInterview 从“知识点仓库”改成“面试作战手册”的核心。面试中不要从工具开始讲，不要一上来堆 Ray / vLLM / Triton / Kafka / Vector DB。更强的讲法是：

Start from the product problem, define the production artifact, then design the data/model/control-plane lifecycle that makes the product reliable.

一个好答案应该每次都能覆盖：

  • Product workflow：用户到底要完成什么任务？
  • Production artifact：系统最终产物是 answer、forecast、image、video、index、model、edit plan，还是 agent action？
  • Data/model lifecycle：输入、版本、评估、回滚、血缘怎么管理？
  • Architecture：control plane / data plane / serving plane 怎么拆？
  • Algorithms：retrieval、ranking、routing、scheduling、batching、sampling、indexing、evaluation 的关键逻辑是什么？
  • Infra and ops：latency、cost、GPU、queue、failure、monitoring、security、tenant isolation 怎么做？
  • My contribution：我和团队分别做了什么，什么设计是我推动/owner 的？

1. 统一答题模板：把每个 AI 系统题讲成一个产品系统#

面试官问任何 AI infra / LLM / multimodal / agent / RAG 设计题时，可以用这个顺序：

  1. Clarify product workflow：谁在用？同步还是异步？interactive 还是 batch？失败时用户能不能等待？
  2. Define artifact lifecycle：artifact 是 model、embedding、index、media output、workflow state、agent action 还是 evaluation report？
  3. Draw architecture：client -> API gateway -> control plane -> scheduler/router -> workers/serving engines -> storage/registry/observability。
  4. Explain request/data flow：一次请求从进入系统到产出结果，每一步怎么记录状态、版本和 trace。
  5. Choose algorithms and stack：为什么用 hybrid retrieval、reranker、continuous batching、priority queue、Ray、vLLM、Triton、Kubernetes。
  6. Discuss trade-offs：latency vs throughput、freshness vs cost、quality vs safety、centralization vs team autonomy、general platform vs product-specific optimization。
  7. Failure and ops：如何 debug、rollback、degrade、alert、capacity plan、做 postmortem。
  8. Contribution angle：我负责 contract、state machine、routing policy、registry、observability schema、deployment gate、cost model、cross-team alignment 中的哪些部分。

一句话版本：

I do not design AI infra as a pile of services. I design the lifecycle of the product artifact: how it is created, versioned, evaluated, served, monitored, rolled back, and trusted by customers.

2. Design Prompt：adt-style Multimodal Asset Intelligence Platform#

Interview prompt

Design a platform that lets image-editing, photo-management, video-editing, lightweight-design, and enterprise users search, understand, tag, and reuse image, video, audio, document, and 3D assets.

30-second answer

我会把它设计成 multimodal asset intelligence platform，核心不是一个 vector DB，而是一条 versioned semantic asset lifecycle：ingestion -> normalization -> modality-specific encoding -> metadata extraction -> hybrid index build -> ACL-aware retrieval -> reranking -> product integration -> feedback/evaluation。产品上支持 search-by-text、search-by-image、similarity search、semantic tagging、related asset discovery、creative reuse。系统上要保证 multi-tenant isolation、delete propagation、index freshness、ranking quality、cost control 和可 debug 的 retrieval lineage。

Requirements

支持 image、video、audio、document、3D asset。 · P95 search latency 目标通常是 hundreds of ms 到 1-2s，离线 indexing 可以是 minutes/hours。 · 企业客户要求 ACL filtering、asset-level permissions、audit log、delete/right-to-be-forgotten。 · 支持 incremental indexing、backfill、embedding model upgrade、index rollback。 · 支持 product signals：click、open、download、insert、edit、share、accept/reject tag。

High-level architecture

text
Asset Upload / Connector
  -> Ingestion Service
  -> Media Normalization Workers
  -> Metadata Extractors
  -> Embedding / Encoder Serving
  -> Index Builder
  -> Vector Index + Lexical Index + Metadata Store
  -> Query Understanding
  -> Candidate Retrieval
  -> ACL Filter
  -> Reranker / Diversifier
  -> Product Result API / SDK
  -> Feedback + Evaluation Store

Core data model

text
Asset(id, tenant_id, type, owner, acl_version, source_uri, checksum, created_at)
AssetDerivative(asset_id, derivative_type, uri, codec, resolution, duration)
Embedding(asset_id, modality, model_version, vector, created_at)
IndexVersion(index_id, model_version, chunk_policy, build_id, status)
SearchTrace(query_id, index_version, candidates, filters, reranker_version, clicked_asset)

Algorithmic depth

  • Image：CLIP-style image encoder，支持 global embedding；对复杂图像可加 object/region embeddings。
  • Video：scene boundary detection + keyframe sampling + audio transcript + OCR + shot-level embeddings。
  • Audio：ASR transcript、speaker/music/sound event features、audio embedding。
  • Document：text extraction、layout-aware chunking、OCR、table extraction。
  • Retrieval：BM25 + vector ANN + metadata filter，再用 cross-encoder reranker 或 lightweight multimodal reranker。
  • Diversification：MMR、source-level dedup、cluster-based selection，避免结果全来自同一 project/folder/scene。
  • Index：HNSW/IVF/PQ/ScaNN/FAISS/Milvus/OpenSearch kNN 的选择取决于 scale、update rate、recall、memory、ops maturity。

Tech stack selection

  • Kafka / Kinesis：asset change event、delete event、reindex event。
  • Flink / Spark / Ray Data：large-scale backfill、embedding generation、feature extraction。
  • Triton / ONNX Runtime / TorchServe：encoder/reranker serving；如果是 batch embedding，Ray workers + GPU pools 更灵活。
  • Milvus / OpenSearch / Elasticsearch / FAISS：vector + lexical retrieval；大规模场景要考虑 shard、replica、compaction、index rebuild。
  • Postgres / DynamoDB：asset metadata、index job state、ACL version。
  • OpenTelemetry + Prometheus + warehouse：trace query path、index freshness、recall/click metrics。

Trade-offs

DecisionOption AOption BHow to answer
Retrievalpure vectorhybrid lexical + vectorEnterprise asset search usually needs exact terms, filenames, metadata, and semantic match; hybrid is safer.
ACLpost-filter topKpre-filter / filter-aware ANNPost-filter is simpler but can destroy recall under strict permissions. At enterprise scale I prefer filter-aware retrieval or retrieve more with bounded cost.
Freshnessreal-time indexingmicro-batch indexingReal-time is expensive and operationally harder; micro-batch with freshness SLO is often enough.
Embedding upgradeoverwriteside-by-side index versionSide-by-side is necessary for eval, rollback, and A/B.

Failure modes and debug

  • Search quality drops：检查 query distribution、embedding model version、index version、chunk/keyframe policy、reranker version、ACL filter、metadata freshness。
  • Latency rises：拆成 query understanding、ANN search、metadata fetch、ACL filter、rerank、network、cache miss。
  • Missing asset：看 ingestion event、normalization、embedding job、index commit、ACL visibility、delete tombstone。
  • Cross-tenant risk：每层都带 tenant_id，storage path、index shard、cache key、trace/log 都不能丢 tenant boundary。

My contribution angle

My contribution would not be “I used a vector DB.” I would say I designed the asset intelligence lifecycle: metadata schema, versioned embedding/index artifacts, hybrid retrieval API, index build state machine, ACL-aware retrieval contract, and observability that lets product and ML teams debug search quality together.

3. Design Prompt：adt-style Creative Generation Workflow Platform#

Interview prompt

Design a backend for image/video/audio generation and editing workflows used by creative products. Users need preview, iteration, safety review, artifact management, and export.

30-second answer

Creative GenAI 不是单次 generate() API。它是一个 artifact lifecycle system：prompt/reference/mask/source asset 进入系统，经过 policy、routing、GPU execution、postprocess、preview、human iteration、final render、provenance、export。架构上我会拆成 workflow control plane、model execution plane、artifact graph、policy/eval plane 和 observability/cost plane。核心目标是让用户快速看到 preview，同时保证 enterprise safety、rights、rollback、cost attribution 和 reproducibility。

High-level architecture

text
Creative App / API
  -> Request Gateway
  -> Prompt + Asset Policy Check
  -> Workflow Orchestrator
  -> Execution Planner
  -> GPU Model Workers / vLLM / Diffusion / Audio / Video Engines
  -> Postprocess / Transcode / Upscale / Safety
  -> Artifact Store + Asset Graph
  -> Preview CDN / Product Callback
  -> Content Credentials / Audit / Billing / Metrics

Workflow state machine

text
CREATED
  -> VALIDATED
  -> QUEUED
  -> RUNNING
  -> PREVIEW_READY
  -> POSTPROCESSING
  -> SAFETY_REVIEW
  -> FINAL_READY
  -> EXPORTED
  -> FAILED / CANCELED / EXPIRED

每个 transition 要有 idempotency key。用户点击 retry、浏览器断线、callback 重复、worker 重启，都不能生成重复收费或覆盖错误版本。

Model and data design

  • Request metadata：prompt、negative prompt、seed、mask、reference asset ids、style preset、brand kit、user intent。
  • Model metadata：model family、version、adapter、safety classifier version、postprocess version。
  • Artifact graph：source asset -> mask -> generated preview -> upscaled final -> exported derivative。
  • Provenance：who/when/model/prompt/reference/policy/export，支持 Content Credentials 或 sidecar metadata。

Algorithmic depth

  • Image generation：diffusion sampling、scheduler、CFG scale、seed reproducibility、mask-guided inpainting。
  • Video generation：keyframe conditioning、temporal consistency、shot segmentation、motion control、frame interpolation、transcoding。
  • Audio：TTS、voice conversion、music/sound event generation、loudness normalization、ASR alignment。
  • Safety：prompt classifier、input asset scan、output classifier、brand policy、rights policy、human review queue。
  • Routing：model capability matrix + latency class + cost budget + tenant policy + GPU availability。

Tech stack selection

  • Temporal / Step Functions / Argo Workflows：long-running workflow、retry、timeout、cancellation。
  • Ray Serve：multi-step model DAG、Python-native orchestration、GPU worker pools。
  • Triton：high-throughput classifier/embedding/upscaler/reranker。
  • Kubernetes + KEDA / custom scheduler：queue length、GPU class、priority、tenant quota。
  • S3 / Blob store + CDN：media artifacts and derivatives。
  • OpenTelemetry：trace across gateway、workflow、GPU, postprocess, artifact store。

Trade-offs

  • Synchronous API 简单，但 video/audio generation 会超时；异步 job API 更适合 long-running media。
  • Preview/final split 可以改善 perceived latency，但要管理 two-stage artifact quality 和用户 expectations。
  • Shared GPU pool 提高利用率，但 enterprise tenant 可能需要 quota/isolation。
  • Full reproducibility 需要 seed/model/config/artifact lineage，但有些 nondeterministic GPU kernels 和 external models 会让 bitwise reproducibility 很难。

Failure modes and ops

  • Queue time high but GPU low：可能是 scheduler bin-packing、model image cold start、GPU memory fragmentation、priority starvation。
  • Safety false positive 高：按 policy type、locale、asset category 分桶看，不要只看 global block rate。
  • Preview ready but export failed：检查 transcode/postprocess/CDN/provenance chain。
  • Cost spike：按 model family、workflow type、tenant、retry reason、cancel-after-start 归因。

My contribution angle

I would frame my contribution as the workflow and artifact layer: state machine, execution planner, model routing policy, artifact graph, observability schema, retry/cancel semantics, and deployment gates. That shows I understand the creative product, not just the GPU backend.

4. Design Prompt：Enterprise RAG and Agent Platform With Governance#

Interview prompt

Design an enterprise AI assistant that answers questions, retrieves internal knowledge, calls tools, and performs actions safely across multiple products and tenants.

30-second answer

我会把它拆成 knowledge plane、agent runtime、tool governance plane 和 evaluation/observability plane。RAG 负责 grounded context，agent 负责 planning/tool use，policy layer 负责 permission、PII、prompt injection、side effects。关键不是让 LLM 会调用工具，而是让每次 retrieval、prompt assembly、tool call、action commit 都有 authorization、trace、rollback 和 eval。

Architecture

text
User / Product Surface
  -> Auth + Tenant Context
  -> Intent Classifier
  -> Retrieval Planner
  -> ACL-aware Retriever
  -> Reranker + Context Packer
  -> Agent Planner
  -> Tool Registry + Policy Engine
  -> Tool Executor / Human Approval
  -> Response Generator
  -> Citation + Action Log + Feedback

RAG workflow

  1. Ingest documents with connector identity and ACL snapshot.
  2. Chunk by semantic section, not only token count.
  3. Generate embeddings and store chunk metadata: source, timestamp, owner, ACL version, retention policy.
  4. Query understanding：rewrite、entity extraction、time filter、product domain detection。
  5. Retrieve hybrid candidates：BM25 + vector + metadata constraints。
  6. Apply ACL filtering before final context assembly。
  7. Rerank and pack context with citations。
  8. Generate answer with grounded citation constraints。
  9. Evaluate faithfulness, answer helpfulness, citation correctness。

Agent tool design

Tool registry 不能只是 function name。每个 tool 需要：

JSON schema and output schema。 · owner、SLO、cost、side effect level。 · required permissions and tenant scope。 · dry-run support。 · idempotency key。 · rollback/compensation behavior。 · audit log fields。 · human approval policy。

Algorithms and guardrails

  • Retrieval：hybrid search、reranking、MMR、query decomposition、metadata filter。
  • Prompt injection defense：separate system/tool/data instructions；retrieved documents are data, not authority。
  • Tool selection：capability routing + permission filtering + risk scoring。
  • Action safety：two-phase pattern：plan -> validate -> approve/commit。
  • Evaluation：golden set、LLM-as-judge with calibration、citation faithfulness、tool-call correctness、side-effect simulation。

Trade-offs

  • Fully autonomous agent feels powerful but is risky；enterprise agent should be approval-aware and reversible。
  • More tools increase capability but consume context and increase attack surface；use domain-specific tool bundles。
  • RAG reduces hallucination but does not eliminate it；must evaluate citation faithfulness and retrieval coverage。
  • Central platform improves governance but product teams need extensibility；use shared runtime + product-specific tools。

Failure modes

  • Wrong answer with correct-looking citation：citation text is relevant but does not support claim。
  • User can retrieve forbidden data：ACL snapshot stale or query-time filter missing。
  • Tool called with wrong tenant：tenant context not propagated into tool executor。
  • Agent loops：missing step budget, bad planner, tool output not normalized。
  • Logs leak data：prompt/context/tool output stored without redaction or retention policy。

My contribution angle

I would say I worked on making agent behavior production-safe: tool registry contract, tenant-aware policy checks, action audit trail, RAG trace schema, evaluation gates, and rollback/human approval paths. That makes me more than an infra person; it shows I can productize AI behavior.

5. Design Prompt：Kubernetes-native LLM Inference Control Plane#

Interview prompt

Design a Kubernetes-native control plane for serving many LLMs with vLLM, SGLang, TensorRT-LLM, and Triton across shared GPU clusters.

30-second answer

我会设计一个 engine-agnostic LLM inference control plane。Kubernetes 负责 pod lifecycle 和 base scheduling，control plane 负责 model lifecycle、engine config、GPU placement、request routing、autoscaling、tenant quota、observability 和 cost。数据面可以是 vLLM/SGLang/TensorRT-LLM/Triton，控制面通过 CRD、router、metrics collector、scheduler extender 或 operator 把它们统一起来。

Architecture

text
ModelDeployment CRD
  -> Inference Operator
  -> Model Artifact Resolver
  -> GPU Placement / Quota Controller
  -> Engine Adapter: vLLM / SGLang / TRT-LLM / Triton
  -> Token-aware Router
  -> Metrics Collector
  -> Autoscaler
  -> Cost / Tenant Policy

CRD sketch

yaml
apiVersion: ai.platform/v1
kind: ModelDeployment
spec:
  model: llama-family/model-x
  engine: vllm
  precision: fp8
  parallelism:
    tensorParallel: 4
    pipelineParallel: 1
  resources:
    gpuType: h100
    gpuCount: 4
  routing:
    maxInputTokens: 32000
    priorityClass: interactive
    tenantQuota: team-a
  autoscaling:
    targetTTFTMs: 800
    targetTPOTMs: 80
    minReplicas: 1
    maxReplicas: 20

Key algorithms

  • Token-aware routing：route by estimated input tokens、output tokens、KV cache pressure、queue length、prefix cache hit probability。
  • Batching：continuous batching improves throughput but can hurt tail latency if priorities are mixed。
  • Autoscaling：use queue time、TTFT、TPOT、KV cache utilization、tokens/sec, not only CPU/GPU utilization。
  • Placement：GPU memory fit, tensor parallel group locality, NVLink topology, MIG constraints, model warm pool。
  • Load shedding：reject/route/degrade based on tenant priority and SLO class。

vLLM / SGLang / TensorRT-LLM / Triton selection

  • vLLM：great general-purpose LLM serving, paged attention, continuous batching, OpenAI-compatible API ecosystem。
  • SGLang：good for structured generation, agentic workloads, prefix/cache-heavy programs。
  • TensorRT-LLM：best when you can invest in engine build, kernel-level optimization, stable model shapes。
  • Triton：strong for non-LLM inference, ensembles, embeddings, classifiers, CV/audio models。

High-performance networking depth

  • Multi-GPU inference depends on NVLink/NVSwitch topology; cross-node tensor parallel adds network cost and is usually avoided unless necessary。
  • NCCL tuning matters for distributed serving/training：IB/RDMA, GPUDirect RDMA, topology-aware placement, avoiding noisy neighbor。
  • Router should avoid moving KV cache across nodes unless using prefill/decode disaggregation or special cache transfer design。
  • For large clusters, control-plane scalability matters：watch cardinality, CRD churn, metrics volume, rollout storms。

Failure modes

  • GPU utilization high but throughput low：decode-bound, memory bandwidth-bound, long outputs, poor batching, low cache hit。
  • GPU utilization low but latency high：queue misrouting, cold model load, scheduler fragmentation, router hot shard。
  • OOM / KV cache full：max tokens too high, bad admission control, prefix cache fragmentation。
  • Autoscaler oscillates：using lagging metrics, cold start too slow, no hysteresis。

My contribution angle

I would emphasize control-plane ownership: CRD/API design, engine adapters, token-aware router, autoscaling signals, GPU quota/isolation, rollout strategy, and ops dashboards. That shows distributed systems depth plus LLM serving awareness.

6. Design Prompt：Per-customer Model Training and Deployment Platform#

Interview prompt

Design a platform where every customer gets a custom forecasting model. The team iterates quickly, but needs reliable deployment, monitoring, security, and cost control.

30-second answer

这是 tenant-aware ML lifecycle platform，不是普通 CI/CD。每个客户的数据、训练配置、模型版本、评估、部署、推理、监控和 rollback 都要有 customer scope。架构上我会把 control plane 管 training/deployment state，把 data plane 跑 ETL/training/inference，把 governance plane 管 isolation、audit、SOC2 evidence，把 ops plane 管 metrics、cost、incident。

Lifecycle

text
Customer onboarded
  -> connector configured
  -> data validated
  -> dataset version created
  -> training job scheduled
  -> model artifact registered
  -> offline eval passed
  -> shadow/canary deployed
  -> production promoted
  -> monitored
  -> retrained / rolled back / retired

Architecture

text
Customer Portal / Admin API
  -> Tenant Control Plane
  -> Data Connector Framework
  -> Validation + Feature Pipeline
  -> Training Orchestrator
  -> Model Registry
  -> Evaluation Gate
  -> Deployment Controller
  -> Inference Service
  -> Monitoring + Cost + Audit

Important design choices

  • Dataset version and model version must be linked；otherwise forecast regression is impossible to debug。
  • Training job must be reproducible：code version、config、data snapshot、feature definition、random seed、container image。
  • Model deployment must support shadow/canary；custom model per tenant makes rollback customer-specific。
  • Monitoring must include product metrics, not only infra：forecast error、drift、user override rate、planner acceptance。
  • Security must be customer-scoped at storage, compute, logs, artifacts, cache, and support tooling。

Algorithms / ML depth

  • Forecasting eval：MAPE/SMAPE/RMSE/MAE, calibration, prediction interval coverage, backtesting by time split。
  • Drift detection：feature distribution shift, seasonality change, missing data spike, label delay。
  • Retraining trigger：schedule-based + drift-based + product feedback + data volume threshold。
  • Model selection：global model + per-customer fine-tune vs per-customer model from scratch vs hierarchical model。

Operational model

  • Daily：failed jobs、stale datasets、expiring credentials、GPU spend、customer-facing errors。
  • Weekly：model quality by tenant, retraining backlog, deployment success rate, support tickets。
  • Monthly：cost per customer, unused models, retention/deletion audit, SOC2 evidence review。

My contribution angle

I would describe how I turned repeated customer ML work into a governed lifecycle: dataset/model registry, training state machine, evaluation gate, deployment rollback, customer-scoped metrics, and cost/security controls. That speaks to product, model, data, infra, and customer trust at the same time.

7. Design Prompt：AI Tech Stack Implementation Project#

Interview prompt

You have Ray, vLLM, Triton, Kubernetes, Kafka, a vector DB, MLflow/W&B, and OpenTelemetry. Design a real production project that connects them coherently.

30-second answer

我会设计一个 AI Workflow Platform，让 product teams 用统一 API 提交 search/generate/analyze/agent tasks。Kubernetes 管资源边界，Ray 管 distributed workflow，vLLM 管 LLM generation，Triton 管 embedding/reranking/classifiers，Kafka 管 async events，vector DB 管 semantic retrieval，MLflow/W&B 管 model/eval metadata，OpenTelemetry 管 trace。关键是每个 stack 有清晰边界，不是所有问题都塞进一个框架。

End-to-end workflow

text
POST /ai/tasks
  -> API Gateway validates tenant/user/request
  -> Intent Router classifies task_type
  -> Workflow Controller creates TaskRun
  -> Ray DAG executes steps
       -> Triton embedding
       -> Vector DB retrieval
       -> Triton reranking
       -> vLLM generation
       -> policy check
       -> artifact write
  -> Kafka emits state events
  -> Product receives callback / streaming response
  -> OTel trace + metrics + cost record

Component boundaries

ComponentOwnsShould not own
Kubernetespod lifecycle, GPU resources, networking, isolationbusiness workflow semantics
Raydistributed Python DAG, task retries, actor poolstenant security policy as the only source of truth
vLLMtoken serving, batching, KV cache, LLM endpointlong-running workflow state
Tritonhigh-throughput model inferenceproduct orchestration
Kafkadurable state/event streamsynchronous request path decisions
Vector DBANN retrieval/index servingsource-of-truth document storage
MLflow/W&Bexperiments, model metadata, eval artifactsruntime authorization
OpenTelemetrytraces/metrics/log correlationhigh-cardinality business analytics alone

Critical implementation contracts

python
class TaskRun:
    task_id: str
    tenant_id: str
    user_id: str
    task_type: str  # search | generate | analyze | agent_action
    state: str
    input_refs: list[str]
    output_refs: list[str]
    model_versions: dict[str, str]
    trace_id: str
    idempotency_key: str

class ExecutionStep:
    step_id: str
    step_type: str  # embed | retrieve | rerank | generate | tool_call
    engine: str     # triton | vllm | ray_actor | external_tool
    retry_policy: dict
    timeout_ms: int
    cost_units: float

How the stacks work together

Ray worker 不直接绕过 gateway 访问用户数据；它拿 scoped artifact reference。 · vLLM request 带 tenant/model/SLO metadata，router 用于 batching 和 quota。 · Triton embedding output 写入 vector DB，同时记录 embedding model version。 · Kafka state events 驱动 callback、audit、billing、offline analytics。 · OpenTelemetry trace 贯穿 API、Ray task、Triton call、vLLM call、vector query、artifact write。 · MLflow/W&B 记录 model/eval；production registry 决定可部署版本。

Painpoints

Ray task retry 和 external side effect 容易重复执行；必须 idempotent。 · vLLM continuous batching 对 tail latency 有影响；需要 priority queues。 · Triton batching 提升吞吐但会增加 p99；interactive 和 batch 要分池。 · Vector DB index update 和 delete propagation 是合规风险。 · OTel label cardinality 不能放 prompt、asset_id、user_id；这些放 trace/log/warehouse。 · Kubernetes GPU fragmentation 会导致有 GPU 但调度不上。

My contribution angle

I would say I designed the contracts between the stacks: task state, idempotency, artifact references, model/index version propagation, trace schema, routing policy, and deployment gates. That is more valuable than simply naming Ray or vLLM.

8. How to practice this section in interviews#

每天练一个 prompt，按下面 7 句话讲：

  1. The product problem is...
  2. The production artifact is...
  3. The lifecycle is...
  4. The architecture has these planes...
  5. The hardest trade-off is...
  6. The failure modes I would monitor are...
  7. My contribution would be...

示例收尾：

The reason I like this design is that it connects product workflow, model quality, infra scalability, and customer trust. It is not just a serving stack; it is a production AI system with lifecycle, observability, and rollback.

Interview Master Playbook / Project Deep Dive Portfolio#

这一节不再堆项目清单，而是作为面试时的项目讲述框架。目标是让面试官听到三件事：你理解 product workflow，你能把 AI/model/data/infra 接成 production system，你能说清楚 tradeoff 和 impact。

1. 90 秒项目讲法#

用一个固定骨架讲，避免散：product problem -> AI/data challenge -> architecture -> hard tradeoff -> impact。例子：

The product problem was that teams wanted to reuse an AI capability across multiple workflows, but each workflow had different latency, privacy, media size, model, and operational requirements. I designed the production systems layer: workflow state, routing policy, model/index/artifact versioning, async execution, observability, and rollback. The hard tradeoff was balancing fast iteration with stable contracts. The impact was faster onboarding of new AI use cases and easier debugging across product, model, data, and infra layers.

2. 三个最值得准备的 flagship projects#

Multimodal AI Execution Platform：讲 intent registry、execution planner、edge-cloud routing、workflow state、artifact lineage、CPU/GPU resource controls。深挖点是为什么 routing 不能只按模型名，而要看 privacy、network、media size、latency target、cost 和 user context。

Semantic Index / RAG / Discovery Platform：讲 ingestion、chunking、embedding、index versioning、ACL-aware retrieval、delete propagation、reranking、recall/latency/freshness eval。深挖点是 index 不是存储系统，而是 versioned retrieval artifact；debug retrieval quality 必须能追到 document version、chunking strategy、embedding model、index build 和 query-time filters。

Creative AI Workflow Platform：讲 asset graph、async generation/editing、preview/final render、model routing、safety status、Content Credentials/provenance、export path。深挖点是 creative AI 不是一次性 API response，而是可编辑、可比较、可撤销、可审计的 production asset lifecycle。

3. 回答贡献时怎么避免 “fake project” 感#

不要说 “I built an AI platform”。要说你 owned 的 concrete surfaces：state machine、API contract、metadata model、scheduler/routing policy、model/index registry integration、trace schema、rollout/rollback、runbook。每个 surface 都能被追问：状态有哪些？哪些操作 idempotent？版本怎么回滚？trace 怎么关联 model/index/artifact？失败如何分类？成本怎么归因？

4. 面试官常见追问和答法#

如果问 “why not simpler?”：先给 baseline，再说明什么时候复杂度才值得引入。例如 single model endpoint 可以做 demo，但当 workflow 需要 async、retry、cancel、progress、artifact lineage、tenant isolation 和 rollback 时，就需要 workflow/control-plane contract。

如果问 “how do you measure success?”：不要只说 uptime。AI product systems 要看 product、quality、infra、ops 四类指标：task completion / accepted asset rate；recall@k / eval pass / safety block quality；p95 latency / queue time / GPU utilization / cost per successful job；MTTR / rollback time / failure taxonomy coverage。

如果问 “what was hard?”：强调 cross-layer ambiguity。AI 失败可能来自 stale data、bad prompt、model regression、index drift、permission filter、GPU queue、media preprocessing 或 UI retry。你的价值是让这些边界通过 lineage、state、observability 和 release gates 变得可定位。

5. 简历和面试的一句话定位#

I build AI product systems: the layer that connects product workflows, data/model lifecycle, semantic retrieval, multimodal execution, deployment safety, customer trust, and daily operations.

adt-style Product / Service 面试定位#

1. 如果面试偏 adt-style creative platform，应该如何重新定位这份准备？#

adt-style AI infra 面试的核心不是只会训练模型，而是理解 creative workflow 和 enterprise content workflow。产品场景覆盖 image、video、audio、design、marketing、document、customer experience。Infra 需要支持低延迟交互式生成、长时间媒体处理、企业级权限、brand safety、content provenance、asset lifecycle、global scale 和 cost control。可以把你的定位说成：I build reliable AI media platforms that turn model capability into production creative workflows。

2. 这类 creative GenAI 产品语境里有哪些关键词？#

这类产品语境里要熟悉 creative suite、design editor、image editor、video editor、marketing platform、AI Assistant、Agent Orchestrator、Content Credentials、creative API services、Generative Fill、Text to Video、Image to Video、Translate audio/video、Dubbing and Lip Sync、partner models。面试中不一定要背产品细节，但要能把这些映射到 infra：model serving、media pipelines、workflow orchestration、security、observability。

3. adt-style AI infra 和普通 LLM app infra 有什么不同？#

普通 LLM app 多数是 text in/text out；adt-style AI infra 是 media-heavy、workflow-heavy、asset-heavy。Image/video/audio 都有大文件上传、transcoding、GPU inference、async job、preview、render artifact、CDN、metadata、版权与 provenance。用户体验也不同：创作者需要 iterative editing、undo/redo、layer/mask/timeline/control handles，而不是一次性 chat answer。Infra 设计必须支持 fast interactive loop 和 durable production asset。

4. 面试中如何展示你理解 creative user？#

可以说：Creative users care about control, iteration, latency, quality, rights, and workflow integration. A generated asset is not just an output blob; it may become a layer in an image editor, a clip in a video editor, a campaign asset in a lightweight design tool, or a marketing variant in an enterprise experience suite. 因此系统要保存 prompt、seed、reference assets、edits、model version、content credentials、review state 和 export metadata。这样用户能重做、比较、审计、协作和发布。

5. adt-style infra 的成功指标是什么？#

不仅是 uptime 和 GPU utilization，还包括 time-to-first-preview、generation success rate、creative acceptance rate、edit iteration count、render completion rate、asset export/share rate、policy block accuracy、cost per accepted asset、model latency by media type、queue wait time、regional availability。对 enterprise marketing，还要看 campaign throughput、brand compliance、localization speed、review cycle reduction。

Image GenAI / Editing System Design#

1. 设计一个 Generative Fill / image editing backend#

用户上传 image，前端提供 mask、prompt、style/reference、尺寸和编辑参数。Backend 创建 job，保存 input asset 到 object storage，做 safety scan 和 format normalization，调用 image generation model，生成多个 candidates，保存 outputs、metadata、model version、seed、prompt hash、content credentials。交互式体验需要低 TTFR，也就是 first preview latency；高质量 render 可以 async 完成。关键是 asset lineage：每个 output 都能追溯到 input、mask、prompt、model 和 policy decision。

2. Image pipeline 的核心组件是什么？#

组件包括 upload service、asset store、metadata DB、preprocessor、moderation service、generation service、candidate store、thumbnail/preview generator、CDN、content credentials signer、audit log、analytics event pipeline。对于 pro image-editing workflow，还要保存 layer/mask/edit graph，而不是只保存 flatten image。企业场景还需要 brand kit、template、font/license、permission 和 review workflow。

3. Image GenAI 最容易出什么 production 问题？#

常见问题包括上传大图超时、格式/色彩空间不一致、EXIF/metadata 泄露、mask 坐标错位、GPU OOM、队列过长、输出违反 policy、内容与 prompt 不符、相似图重复、版权/商用安全争议。Infra 应对方式是 input normalization、size limits、async jobs、retry/idempotency、safety filters、artifact versioning、human review hooks、observability。

4. 如何支持 iterative image editing？#

需要把每次 edit 当成 versioned operation：base asset、mask、prompt、model version、parameters、output candidates。前端可以展示 edit history 和 branches，后端保存 edit graph。这样用户能 undo、redo、compare、fork。Infra 上要控制 storage growth，可以用 derivative retention policy、dedup、compression、lifecycle rules。这个问题很适合展示你理解 creative workflow。

5. 如何做 image generation 的安全和合规？#

输入和输出都要 policy scan，包括 harmful content、PII、copyright-sensitive content、brand misuse、deepfake/persona 风险。企业模式下还要 brand safety 和 license constraints。输出要附带 provenance metadata 或 Content Credentials。日志中不要保存用户原始素材，除非有明确 consent 和 access control。安全不是后置 filter，而是贯穿 upload、generation、storage、sharing。

Video GenAI / Editing System Design#

1. 设计 Text to Video / Image to Video 服务#

Video generation 通常是 async job。用户提交 prompt、reference image/keyframes、duration、aspect ratio、style、model。系统先做 policy check 和 asset normalization，进入 GPU job queue，生成 low-res preview，再生成 final clip，之后做 encoding/transcoding、thumbnail、metadata、content credentials、CDN delivery。需要 job status API、progress events、cancel/retry、idempotency key 和 quota。核心难点是 video generation expensive and long-running。

2. Video infra 和 image infra 的差异是什么？#

Video 多了时间维度、帧序列、音轨、codec、container、transcoding、timeline、partial preview、长任务调度和更高存储成本。Image 可以同步返回候选，video 多数需要 async queue。Video 的 quality eval 也更复杂：temporal consistency、motion quality、flicker、object identity、camera motion、audio sync。Infra 要把 generation、render、transcode、delivery 拆成 pipeline。

3. 如何设计 video job queue？#

Job metadata 包括 user_id、tenant_id、input assets、duration、resolution、model、priority、quota、estimated GPU cost、status、retry count。Queue 支持 priority、fairness、cancellation、preemption、deadline、GPU type routing。Worker 从 queue 拉任务，定期 checkpoint/progress update。失败要区分 policy block、user input error、GPU OOM、transient infra、model failure。Video 成本高，所以必须有 quota 和 cost attribution。

4. 如何降低 video generation 的 perceived latency？#

先返回 job accepted 和 estimated time；尽快生成低分辨率 preview 或 first segment；支持 progressive preview；把 transcode 和 final packaging 放到后面；用 notification 或 websocket/SSE 更新进度。对常见 prompt/template 做 cache 或 warm pool。用户体验上，preview 快比 final 快更重要，因为创作者会迭代。

5. Video 输出如何做 Content Credentials 和 provenance？#

记录 prompt、reference assets、model version、generation time、edit operations、AI-generated flag、transcode chain、signing metadata。输出 MP4/WebM 或 project asset 时附带 provenance 或 sidecar metadata。对于 enterprise workflow，还要保留 audit trail：谁生成、谁编辑、谁批准、何时发布。这个点能连接这类平台的 trust/provenance 方向。

6. 如何设计 Dubbing and Lip Sync / Translate video pipeline？#

Pipeline 可以拆成 speech extraction、ASR、translation、voice generation/voice preservation、lip sync generation、audio mixing、video render、quality check。每一步都要保存 intermediate artifacts 和 timestamps。关键指标是 translation quality、speaker consistency、lip sync error、audio-video alignment、processing time、policy compliance。多语言本地化场景还要支持 batch processing 和 human review。

Audio GenAI / Speech System Design#

1. 设计 Text to Speech 或 voiceover service#

输入 text、voice/style、language、speed、emotion、pronunciation dictionary。系统做 text normalization、policy check、TTS inference、audio post-processing、loudness normalization、format conversion、content credentials。输出可以是 preview + final WAV/MP3。企业场景要管理 voice rights、brand voice、consent、watermarking 和 audit log。

2. 设计 Sound Effects Generator#

用户输入 prompt、duration、category、intensity、loopable flag。系统生成多个 candidates，做 loudness normalization、safety check、dedup、metadata tagging。对 video editor，要能把音效对齐 timeline，并支持 trim/fade/loop。Infra 上 sound effect 生成比 video 轻，但需要大量快速 iteration，所以 cache、candidate management 和低延迟很重要。

3. Audio pipeline 主要监控什么？#

看 job success rate、generation latency、audio duration、format conversion failures、loudness distribution、policy block rate、user accept/export rate。语音类还要看 WER、translation quality、speaker similarity、lip sync alignment、language-specific failure。不要只看服务是否 200，媒体质量和用户采用率才是真正信号。

4. Audio safety 有哪些特殊点？#

Voice cloning、impersonation、consent、deepfake、敏感文本朗读都是风险。需要 voice enrollment controls、consent records、speaker restrictions、watermark/provenance、abuse detection、rate limits。对企业 brand voice，要限制谁能使用、在哪里使用、是否可导出。面试里可以说：audio GenAI 的 security boundary 包含 identity and consent, not just data access。

Creative API Services / Platform#

1. 如果设计类似 creative API services 的 enterprise API，会怎么做？#

核心是把 creative generative capabilities 暴露成 secure, scalable APIs。API 层提供 auth、tenant quota、idempotency、request validation、job creation、status polling/webhook、artifact delivery。Model layer 负责 image/video/audio generation。Enterprise layer 负责 brand kit、templates、content credentials、review workflow、audit logs、usage/cost reporting。这个设计强调 platformization：同一能力可被 image editor、lightweight design tool、enterprise experience suite 或第三方系统调用。

2. API 应该同步还是异步？#

Image 小图 preview 可以同步或短轮询；video、batch localization、high-res render 应该 async。异步 API 需要 POST /jobs、GET /jobs/{id}、cancel、webhook、idempotency key、retry semantics、artifact expiry。面试中要说明：媒体 GenAI 的 latency distribution 长尾明显，用 async job model 能保护 API reliability。

3. Creative API 的 quota 如何设计？#

Quota 不应只按 request count，而要按 compute units：media type、resolution、duration、model、priority、batch size。Video 5 秒和 image thumbnail 成本完全不同。需要 per-tenant quota、burst、monthly budget、rate limit、concurrency limit、abuse detection。用户界面可以显示 credits 或 usage，平台侧要能做 cost attribution。

4. 如何设计 enterprise asset delivery？#

Generated assets 存 object storage，生成 signed URL 或通过 asset service 下载。对大企业要支持 region、retention、DLP、watermark/provenance、access control、expiration。CDN 加速 public/approved assets，private drafts 不能随便 CDN public cache。每个 asset 的 metadata 要包含 tenant、owner、license、content credential、source lineage。

5. API observability 怎么做？#

每个 request/job 都有 request_id、tenant_id、model_id、media_type、cost_units、latency、queue_time、status、policy_result、artifact_id。Dashboard 按 tenant、model、region、media type 分解。Alert 包括 error spike、queue backlog、GPU saturation、policy service down、artifact delivery failure、cost spike。Enterprise API 最怕 black box，因为客户会问每个 job 为什么失败。

LLM / Agent for Creative Workflows#

1. adt-style agent 和普通 chatbot 有什么不同？#

Creative agent 不只是回答问题，而是能理解 project context、assets、brand guidelines、timeline/layers、目标渠道，然后调用工具执行工作。它可能调用 image generation、background removal、video trim、caption、translate、export、campaign variant generation。核心是 agent orchestration around creative tools。回答时可以强调：agent output should be editable, reviewable, and reversible。

2. 设计一个 creative assistant agent#

Agent 输入用户目标：“做一组面向 TikTok/Instagram 的产品宣传素材”。系统读取 brand kit、已有 assets、campaign brief，planner 拆成 tasks：generate background、create copy variants、resize、make video clip、add voiceover、export formats。每一步调用 tool，保存 intermediate assets 和 decision trace。用户可以 approve/reject each step。安全上限制 tool permissions，所有 side effects 可撤销。

3. Agent tool registry 应该包含什么？#

每个 tool 有 name、description、JSON schema、permissions、cost estimate、latency estimate、side_effect level、input/output artifact types、rollback behavior。比如 generate_image、remove_background、translate_video、create_caption、export_asset。Agent planner 只能调用当前用户/tenant 有权限的 tools。Tool registry 是 agent safety 和 extensibility 的核心。

4. Agent memory 在 creative workflow 中怎么设计？#

短期 memory 保存当前 project、selected assets、user intent、recent edits。长期 memory 保存 brand preferences、approved style、team guidelines，但必须可见、可编辑、可删除。Asset memory 应引用 asset_id 和 metadata，而不是把大文件塞进 prompt。企业场景下 memory 必须遵守 tenant isolation 和 retention policy。

5. 如何防止 agent 乱改用户作品？#

所有 destructive operations 都要显式 confirmation 或生成新 version。Agent 默认创建 draft，不覆盖 source asset。对发布、删除、发送、购买、外部分享等 side effects 必须 human approval。系统要有 undo/rollback 和 audit trail。面试里可以说：creative agents should be powerful but non-destructive by default。

6. Agent 如何做 evaluation？#

分 task success、tool correctness、plan quality、user acceptance、safety violation、latency/cost。用 scenario-based eval：给定 brand kit 和 assets，看 agent 是否生成正确尺寸、符合品牌、引用正确素材、没有越权。LLM-as-judge 可以辅助，但最终要有 human creative review 和 product metrics。Agent eval 不能只看单轮回答。

adt Product / Service System Design 题库#

1. 设计 image editor 的 GenAI backend#

重点讲 interactive latency、layer/mask/edit graph、candidate generation、asset versioning、GPU autoscaling、content credentials、undo/redo、policy scan。Architecture：client sends mask + prompt + asset reference，backend normalizes input，generation service returns candidates，asset service stores outputs，metadata service records lineage，analytics captures acceptance. 风险：mask mismatch、large files、policy false positives、GPU queue latency。

2. 设计 video editor 的 AI video workflow backend#

重点讲 timeline-aware processing。服务要支持 clip upload、transcode/proxy generation、scene detection、caption/ASR、translate/dub/lip sync、B-roll generation、export render。长任务用 async job graph，支持 partial failure 和 resume。Metrics 看 render success、queue time、per-stage latency、timeline export rate。安全看 voice consent、content provenance、asset permissions。

3. 设计 lightweight design tool 的 brand-safe content generation#

Express 面向更广泛用户和企业 marketers，所以要强调 template、brand kit、approved fonts/colors/logos、content resizing、多渠道 export。GenAI 输出必须符合 brand constraints，支持 review workflow。Infra 上要有 template service、brand rules engine、generation service、asset approval state、collaboration permissions、usage analytics。

4. 设计 enterprise experience suite 的 campaign asset generation agent#

Agent 根据 campaign brief、audience segment、channel、brand guidelines 生成多版本素材和 copy。它调用 adt-style image/video/audio tools，并把结果送到 review/publish workflow。系统要和 customer data、journey orchestration、asset management、analytics 连接。关键是 enterprise governance：approval、audit、A/B test、personalization、compliance。

5. 设计 AI-powered media search#

输入是 text query、image reference、timeline context 或 brand filters。Index 包含 visual embeddings、audio transcripts、OCR、metadata、tags、rights、project hierarchy。Retrieval 用 hybrid search：keyword + embedding + metadata filter + reranker。输出要支持 permissions、preview thumbnails、source context。指标包括 Recall@K、click/insert rate、search latency、zero-result rate。

6. 设计 Content Credentials / provenance service#

每个生成或编辑 artifact 记录 provenance event：source assets、edit operation、model/tool、timestamp、actor、tenant、signature。服务生成 tamper-evident metadata，可嵌入文件或 sidecar。需要 key management、verification API、privacy controls、export compatibility。面试要强调 trust：用户和企业需要知道内容如何产生，但也不能泄露不该公开的内部素材。

Image / Video / Audio Infra 深挖#

1. 大文件上传如何设计？#

使用 multipart upload、resumable upload、client-side checksum、signed URL、virus/malware scan、media validation。上传完成后发 event 进入 processing pipeline。不要让 API server 直接接收超大文件并同步处理。对 enterprise，要做 tenant quota、file type policy、DLP、region routing。失败要支持 resume，而不是让用户重传 20GB 视频。

2. Media preprocessing 包括什么？#

Image：format conversion、resize、color profile、EXIF stripping、thumbnail。Video：transcode、proxy generation、frame extraction、scene detection、audio extraction、duration/resolution validation。Audio：sample rate conversion、loudness normalization、speech segmentation。Preprocessing 的输出要 versioned，因为模型输入变化会影响结果。

3. Transcoding pipeline 怎么设计？#

上传原始视频后，transcode service 生成 proxy、preview、streaming format 和 final export。可以用 job queue + worker fleet + object storage。支持 partial retry：某个 rendition 失败不一定重跑全部。监控 codec failure、duration、queue time、worker utilization、output size。对用户来说，proxy 快速可编辑，final export 可以慢一点。

4. 如何处理 GPU 和 CPU pipeline 的衔接？#

媒体系统通常 CPU 做 decode/encode/preprocess，GPU 做 model inference。瓶颈可能在 CPU codec、storage IO、network、GPU queue。要用 stage-level metrics 看每一步耗时。优化包括 parallel decode、local NVMe scratch、batching、GPU warm pool、separate CPU/GPU queues。不要只盯 GPU utilization。

5. 如何做 media artifact lifecycle management？#

资产分 raw input、intermediate、preview、final output、published asset。不同类型 retention 不同：intermediate 可短期，final/published 长期，debug artifact 严格受控。用 object storage lifecycle policy、dedup、compression、cold storage。企业客户还要支持 deletion request、legal hold、region policy。存储成本在 media AI 中会非常真实。

Project Stories for adt-style Interviews#

这一节只保留可真实展开的项目叙事。面试时不要把下面内容说成“我一个人完整做过所有系统”；更可信的讲法是：I owned a production slice and collaborated with product, ML, frontend, infra, security, and SRE owners. 每个项目都按 problem -> owned surface -> hard tradeoff -> impact 讲。

1. Project：从 research model 到 production GenAI workflow#

核心叙事：research/demo model 在 notebook 里能跑，但 production creative workflow 需要 multi-tenant auth、async job、quota、retry/cancel、progress、artifact lineage、model version pinning、safety gate、observability 和 rollback。我的可信 contribution 是把一次模型调用变成可运营的 workflow contract：job state machine、request schema、artifact metadata、model version、failure taxonomy、trace/job id、runbook。

深挖回答：为什么 async？因为 image/video/audio generation latency 长尾明显，HTTP sync 会 timeout，也无法表达 progress/cancel/retry。为什么 artifact lineage？因为 creative tools 需要 undo/redo/branch/compare/export，不是只保存最终 blob。为什么 model version pinning？同一个 prompt 在不同模型上结果不同，用户 asset 必须可解释、可复现、可审计。指标看 time-to-first-preview、job success rate、generation p95/p99、retry rate、policy block rate、cost per accepted asset。

2. Project：media ingestion and preprocessing pipeline#

核心叙事：Creative AI 的输入不是干净文本，而是 JPEG/PNG/PSD/PDF/MP4/MOV/WAV/AAC、layer、mask、timeline、font、metadata、color profile、codec 和大文件上传。我的可信 contribution 是把不稳定 input 变成 model-ready、traceable、recoverable 的 asset workflow：signed/multipart upload、checksum、validation、metadata extraction、EXIF stripping、thumbnail/proxy、transcode/normalize、stage-level state、DLQ/replay。

深挖回答：为什么先 normalize？模型支持的格式有限，canonical representation 能降低 worker 复杂度。为什么 proxy/preview？创作者需要快速反馈，不必一开始等 full-resolution final render。为什么 stage-level metrics？整体 latency 没意义，必须知道 upload、decode、transcode、GPU inference、postprocess、delivery 哪一段慢。常见坑包括 color profile mismatch、video/audio timestamp drift、large fan-out、retry 幂等和 tenant-specific retention/deletion。

3. Project：GPU cost and routing without hurting creative UX#

核心叙事：GenAI 功能增长后，GPU bill 和 queue wait 都会变成 product problem。不要只说“我优化 GPU utilization”；更可信的是讲 cost attribution + routing + scheduling 的闭环：把 GPU seconds、model version、feature、tenant、job type、artifact size、accepted/exported signal 关联起来，再按 interactive/batch、preview/final、tenant SLA、latency budget 路由到不同 pool 或 model。

深挖回答：为什么看 cost per accepted asset？只看 cost per generation 会鼓励便宜但低质量的模型；accepted/exported asset 更接近 product value。为什么 preview/final 两阶段？preview 追求 speed，final 追求 quality；两者不一定用同一模型和 GPU policy。为什么不能所有请求都 batch？batching 提高 throughput 但增加 queue wait，interactive editing 需要 max wait 和 warm pool。指标看 queue wait、GPU idle burn、retry waste、cost per accepted asset、abandonment by stage、model fallback rate。

4. 如何避免项目听起来 fake#

少讲“全栈全都我做”，多讲 owned boundary。好的句式是：The team owned the end-to-end product outcome. I owned the platform slice around workflow state, routing, metadata, observability, and rollout. 对任何项目都准备 4 个可追问细节：state machine 有哪些状态；metadata schema 如何关联 model/index/artifact；失败怎么分类和重试；指标如何证明 product impact 而不只是 infra activity。

5. System Design Deep Dive：三个 adt-style flagship projects#

这一节用来把项目故事讲成真正的 system design。面试时不要只说 “built workflow / built pipeline / optimized GPU”。更强的结构是：requirements -> architecture -> data model -> workflow -> implementation -> failure/ops -> metrics。

Project A：Research model to production GenAI workflow#

Requirements：用户在 image editor / lightweight design tool / video editor workflow 里发起 generation/editing，需要 fast preview、progress、cancel、retry、editable output、final export、enterprise audit。Non-goals：不要把 notebook demo 直接包成 sync API；不要把所有 media blob 塞进 request body。

Architecture：

  • Product API：接收 prompt、asset references、mask/layer/timeline context、tenant/user、quality mode。
  • Workflow control plane：管理 job state、retry/cancel、progress、failure taxonomy、idempotency。
  • Model router：按 media type、latency tier、cost tier、tenant policy、model capability 选择 image/video/audio/LLM service。
  • Artifact service：保存 raw input、preview、intermediate、final output、sidecar metadata。
  • Safety/provenance service：policy scan、Content Credentials、model/provider/version trace。
  • Observability：workflow trace + stage metrics + product acceptance signal。

Data model：

  • GenerationJob(job_id, tenant_id, project_id, user_id, state, priority, model_profile, cost_estimate, created_at)。
  • GenerationStep(step_id, job_id, type, input_artifacts, output_artifacts, worker_version, retry_count, error_code, latency_ms)。
  • Artifact(artifact_id, tenant_id, uri, media_type, checksum, parent_artifacts, provenance_id, retention_policy)。
  • ModelInvocation(job_id, step_id, provider, model_name, model_version, prompt_version, seed, params_hash, safety_result)。

Implementation workflow：

  1. Client creates job with asset references, not raw files.
  2. API validates tenant, quota, asset permissions, media type, and requested operation.
  3. Workflow creates idempotency key from tenant_id + project_id + input_hash + operation.
  4. Preprocess workers generate model-ready inputs and lightweight previews.
  5. Model router selects provider/runtime and records model version.
  6. GPU worker runs generation/editing and writes output artifact.
  7. Safety/provenance steps run before result is visible or exportable.
  8. Client polls or subscribes to progress; final output is inserted as editable layer/clip/asset.

Failure / ops：

  • GPU timeout：mark step retryable，但不重复 upload 和 preprocess。
  • Safety uncertain：output stays blocked or goes to review queue。
  • Export metadata missing：block publish，不只是 log warning。
  • User cancel：workflow records cancellation，worker 在 safe checkpoint 停止，temporary artifacts 由 lifecycle job 清理。
  • Model regression：按 model_profile / tenant / product surface 回滚 route。

Metrics：time-to-first-preview、job success rate、p95/p99 per stage、cancel rate、retry waste、policy block rate、accepted/exported asset rate、cost per accepted asset。

Project B：Media ingestion and preprocessing pipeline#

Requirements：用户上传 image、video、audio、document、PSD/layered assets，平台要把不干净、不稳定、大体积输入转成 model-ready representation。Success 不是 upload 成功，而是 downstream model/search/edit/export 都能可靠使用。

Architecture：

  • Upload gateway：resumable/multipart upload、signed URL、checksum、tenant-aware path。
  • Validation service：MIME sniffing、virus scan、size/duration/resolution limits、policy check。
  • Metadata extractor：EXIF、codec、duration、color profile、dimensions、audio tracks、document layout。
  • Preprocess workers：thumbnail/proxy、transcode、frame extraction、OCR/ASR、scene/shot detection。
  • Asset registry：canonical asset state、derived artifacts、lineage、retention。
  • Event bus：asset_uploaded、asset_validated、proxy_ready、embedding_ready、failed。

Implementation workflow：

text
upload -> checksum -> validation -> metadata extraction
       -> canonicalization -> proxy/thumbnail/transcode
       -> optional OCR/ASR/frame extraction
       -> registry update -> downstream index/model workflow

Key choices：

大文件不经过 API server 转发，用 signed URL 直上 object storage。 · preprocessing 输出要 versioned，因为 FFmpeg/libvips/OCR model 版本都会影响下游结果。 · video 要把 proxy generation 和 final transcode 分开：前者保护 UX，后者可以 batch。 · DLQ 不只存 error message，要存 input metadata、worker version、stage、retryable flag。 · ACL/delete propagation 从 asset registry 开始，同步到 thumbnail、proxy、embedding、index、cache。

Pain points：color profile mismatch、video timestamp drift、corrupt media、huge fan-out、tenant-specific retention、partial retry、codec/device compatibility。

Metrics：upload success、validation reject reason、preprocess p95/p99、queue lag by media type、proxy-ready time、DLQ rate、storage cost by artifact class、delete propagation lag。

Project C：GPU cost and routing without hurting creative UX#

Requirements：interactive preview 不能因为 batch job 变慢，enterprise tenant 需要 predictable SLA，平台要知道 GPU spend 到底换来了多少 accepted creative output。

Architecture：

  • Request classifier：interactive preview / final export / batch enrichment / eval / retry。
  • Model capability registry：quality tier、latency tier、GPU type、cost profile、supported media。
  • Routing policy engine：tenant SLA、queue wait、GPU pressure、model availability、fallback policy。
  • Capacity pools：warm interactive pool、throughput batch pool、spot/best-effort pool、dedicated enterprise pool。
  • Cost attribution：job_id -> model -> GPU seconds -> artifact -> accepted/exported event。

Routing logic：

text
score = slo_penalty(queue_wait, tenant_tier)
      + cost_weight * estimated_gpu_cost
      - quality_weight * expected_acceptance
      - locality_weight * warm_model_or_cache_hit
      + risk_penalty(policy_or_model_uncertainty)

Operational controls：

warm pool min/max by product surface and time of day。 · max queue wait for interactive job。 · batch preemption or pause during launch/live traffic。 · fallback from expensive final model to cheaper preview model for low-risk preview。 · budget alarms by tenant/model/product feature。 · cost anomaly detection when a model version raises retry/regeneration rate。

Tradeoffs：higher GPU utilization can hurt interactive p99；cheaper model can increase regeneration and lower acceptance；too many pools reduce bin-packing efficiency；too aggressive fallback can damage user trust。

Metrics：queue wait by class、GPU idle burn、SLO-compliant utilization、cost per accepted asset、preview-to-export conversion、fallback rate、retry waste、tenant budget burn。

Asset Graph、Project Model 与 Collaboration#

1. 为什么 adt-style 产品需要 asset graph？#

Creative work 不是一次请求生成一个文件，而是一连串 editable operations。一个最终视频可能来自原始素材、生成背景、音频、字幕、翻译、裁剪、调色、模板和多次人工修改。Asset graph 记录每个 artifact 的 parents、operations、model/tool version、actor、timestamp 和 permissions。它让 undo/redo、branching、provenance、debug、review、billing 和版权审计都变得可行。

2. 如何设计 project / asset 数据模型？#

可以把 project 看成 container，包含 assets、compositions、timeline/layers、versions、comments、review state。Asset 表存 immutable blob metadata：asset_id、tenant_id、type、uri、checksum、owner、created_by、rights、content_credentials。Operation 表存 edit/generation action：input assets、parameters、tool/model version、output assets。这样既支持 creative graph，也支持权限和审计。

3. 如何支持多人协作编辑？#

需要 project-level permission、presence、locking 或 optimistic concurrency、comment/review、version history。图片 layer 或视频 timeline 可以用 operation log 方式保存，每次修改生成新 revision。冲突处理取决于对象粒度：文本/参数可以 merge，像素/时间线冲突可能需要 branch。企业场景还要 approval workflow：draft -> review -> approved -> published。

4. 如何做 undo/redo 和 branching？#

不要只覆盖文件，而是保存 operation history。Undo 可以回到前一个 revision，redo 重放 operation；branch 可以从任意 revision fork 出新版本。对于昂贵生成结果，不一定每次重算，可以保存 output artifact，并记录 seed/model/input。这样用户体验像 creative tool，而不是一次性 AI endpoint。存储上用 lifecycle policy 管 intermediate artifacts。

5. 如何处理 asset 权限继承？#

Project、folder、asset、derived asset 都要有权限规则。默认 derived asset 继承 parent asset 的权限和 license constraints；如果多个 parent 权限不同，取更严格的 intersection。Agent 或 batch job 生成的资产也必须绑定 tenant/project/actor。权限不能只存在 UI 层，download、preview、search、model context assembly 都要检查。

6. 如何让 generated asset 可审计但不泄露隐私？#

审计需要记录 source、operation、model、time、actor，但不一定公开所有 prompt 和 source asset。可以把 provenance 分成 public metadata 和 private audit log。公开 Content Credentials 说明 AI involvement 和基本来源，私有 audit log 保留详细 lineage。这样兼顾 trust 和 enterprise confidentiality。

Creative Workflow Orchestration#

1. 为什么 media GenAI 需要 workflow engine？#

复杂任务往往不是单个 model call，而是 DAG：upload -> scan -> preprocess -> generate -> safety -> transcode -> credentials -> review -> publish。Workflow engine 能管理 dependency、retry、timeout、partial failure、state、audit。可以用 Temporal、Step Functions、Argo Workflows、Airflow 或自研 lightweight orchestrator。选择取决于任务时长、吞吐、状态复杂度和团队熟悉度。

2. Temporal / Step Functions / Airflow 怎么选？#

Temporal 适合长时间业务 workflow、可靠 retry、复杂状态和代码式 orchestration；Step Functions 和 AWS 集成好，适合 cloud-native serverless/job orchestration；Airflow 偏 batch data pipeline 和调度，不适合高交互用户请求。adt-style media workflow 常是用户触发、长任务、需要取消/重试/状态查询，所以 Temporal 或 Step Functions 会比 Airflow 更自然。

3. 如何设计 workflow state？#

每个 workflow 有 workflow_id、tenant_id、project_id、input asset、steps、status、current stage、retry count、cost estimate、created_by。每个 step 记录 input/output artifact、worker version、start/end time、error category。状态要可查询、可恢复、可审计。用户侧看到的是 progress；工程侧看到的是 stage-level bottleneck。

4. Partial failure 怎么处理？#

如果 video generation 成功但 transcode 某个 rendition 失败，不一定重跑生成；如果 safety scan 失败，要 block output；如果 thumbnail 失败，可以降级但不影响下载。Workflow 要定义哪些 step 是 required，哪些是 optional。这样能避免昂贵任务因为低价值步骤失败而全部重来。

5. 如何支持 cancellation？#

用户取消 video generation 或 batch job 时，control plane 标记 cancel requested，worker 在 safe checkpoint 检查并停止，释放 GPU/CPU 资源，清理 temporary artifacts。已经产生的 billable compute 要记录。取消不是简单 kill process，因为要保证 artifact state 和 audit log 一致。

Model Evaluation for Image / Video / Audio#

1. Image generation 如何评估？#

自动指标包括 aesthetic score、CLIP similarity、object presence、OCR correctness、safety classifier、duplicate detection；但 creative quality 很难完全自动化。更可靠的是 human preference、task-specific acceptance、edit/export rate。面试里可以说：offline eval catches regressions, online creative acceptance validates usefulness。对企业还要测 brand compliance。

2. Video generation 如何评估？#

Video 要看 prompt alignment、temporal consistency、flicker、object identity、motion realism、camera control、duration stability、artifact rate。自动评估可以用 VQA/video judge/model-based metrics，但必须配 human review。线上指标包括 preview acceptance、regeneration rate、export rate、processing failure。Video eval 的难点是质量维度多、样本成本高。

3. Audio generation 如何评估？#

TTS 看 pronunciation、naturalness、speaker similarity、emotion control、latency；translation/dubbing 看 WER、BLEU/COMET、speaker preservation、lip sync offset、human MOS。Sound effects 看 prompt alignment、loop quality、noise、loudness。音频质量强依赖人耳，所以要结合 human rating 和 objective checks。

4. Agent workflow 如何评估？#

Agent eval 需要 scenario-level 测试：给定 project、assets、brand kit、目标，agent 是否能完成任务、调用正确 tools、遵守权限、生成可编辑资产。指标包括 task completion、tool error、unnecessary tool calls、human intervention rate、cost、latency、安全违规。不要只评估回答文本，要评估 workflow outcome。

5. 如何做 model regression gate？#

新模型进入 production 前要跑 golden prompts、adversarial prompts、brand scenarios、media format suite、safety suite、latency/cost benchmarks。Gate 不要求新模型所有指标都赢，但要明确 tradeoff 和 rollout plan。高风险变化先 shadow/canary，按 media type 和 user cohort 放量。Regression gate 是模型发布的 CI。

Brand Safety、Enterprise Governance 与 Rights#

1. Brand-safe generation 怎么设计？#

企业客户需要输出符合 brand kit：logo usage、color palette、font、tone、legal disclaimers、产品图规范。系统可以把 brand kit 作为 retrieval/context/tool constraint，generation 后用 brand validator 检查。对 Express/marketing workflow，brand safety 是核心产品价值，而不是附加 filter。输出不合格时应给可修复 feedback。

2. Rights management 在 creative AI 中为什么重要？#

素材可能有 license、territory、expiration、usage channel、talent rights、music rights。生成或编辑资产时，derived asset 应继承 rights constraints。发布前要检查目标渠道和地区是否允许。Infra 上需要 rights metadata、policy engine、audit log。否则 AI 会让内容生产更快，也让违规传播更快。

3. 如何设计 policy engine？#

Policy engine 输入 tenant、user、asset metadata、operation、target channel、region，输出 allow/deny/review。可以用 OPA/Cedar/自研规则。规则包括 data residency、asset license、brand rules、AI model provider restrictions、export restrictions。Policy decision 要记录用于审计。Agent 和 API 都必须走同一个 policy layer。

4. Human review workflow 怎么做？#

生成资产进入 draft，用户或 reviewer approve/reject/request changes。系统记录 review comments、decision、reviewer、timestamp。高风险内容或低 confidence policy decision 自动进入 review queue。企业营销场景中，review workflow 常比模型本身更决定上线速度。Infra 要支持状态机和通知。

5. 如何处理 commercial safety 的产品表达？#

不要承诺绝对无风险，而要说明训练数据、policy controls、provenance、review workflow、customer controls。对企业客户，重点是可治理：哪些模型可用、哪些素材可用、谁能发布、如何审计。面试里可以说：commercial safety is not only model training; it is an end-to-end governance system。

Creative Ecosystem、Plugin 与 Extensibility#

1. 为什么 extensibility 对 adt-style 平台重要？#

这类产品生态很大，能力需要被 image editor、video editor、design tool、enterprise suite、third-party partners 复用。Extensible platform 可以让新模型、新工具、新 workflow 快速接入，而不重写每个产品。关键是统一 asset API、tool registry、auth、quota、observability、policy。否则每条产品线会长出一套孤立系统。

2. 如何设计 plugin/tool API？#

Plugin 定义 input/output schema、capabilities、permissions、cost class、latency class、supported media types、version。平台负责 auth、sandbox、rate limit、logging、policy enforcement。对 creative tools，输出必须是 editable artifact 或 recognized media asset，而不是随意 blob。Tool API 要支持 capability discovery，让 agent/planner 知道什么时候能用。

3. Third-party model 接入有哪些风险？#

风险包括 data governance、latency variability、cost、availability、policy mismatch、output license、model version drift、regional restriction。平台要有 provider abstraction、per-tenant allowlist、routing policy、fallback、contract tests、observability。每个 output 要记录 provider/model version，方便追溯和客户解释。

4. 如何做 marketplace-like ecosystem 的安全？#

第三方 plugin 需要 review、scopes、sandbox、network restrictions、secret isolation、rate limits、audit logs。用户授权要细粒度，不能让 plugin 默认访问所有 assets。高风险 plugin 操作需要 confirmation。企业管理员要能禁用 plugin 或限制到 approved list。这个方向很容易被安全面试追问。

Edge、Desktop 与 Cloud Hybrid AI#

1. 为什么 desktop creative 产品需要 cloud + local hybrid？#

image editor/video editor 这类 desktop 产品有本地大文件、低延迟交互、专业工作流；GenAI 模型又常需要 cloud GPU。Hybrid 设计让轻量预览、本地编辑、缓存和 UI 保持流畅，把重模型推理放云端。挑战是网络不稳定、大文件上传、版本一致性、离线体验、隐私和权限。面试里可以说：local app owns interaction, cloud owns heavy compute。

2. 哪些 AI 能力适合本地？#

轻量分类、mask refinement、thumbnail、basic enhancement、small on-device models、privacy-sensitive preprocessing 适合本地。大规模 text-to-video、high-res generation、multi-model orchestration 更适合云端。选择取决于 latency、compute、privacy、cost、model size。未来很多产品会是 hybrid model cascade。

3. 如何设计 desktop-to-cloud job flow？#

Desktop client 先本地生成 proxy/preview 和 metadata，使用 resumable upload 传 asset 或 selected region，cloud job 处理后返回 candidates/patch/layer。Client 把结果作为新 layer 或 clip 插入 project。需要处理断网、取消、重试、版本冲突、credential expiry。Job request 应引用 asset hash 和 edit context，避免坐标或版本错位。

4. 如何减少上传成本和隐私风险？#

只上传必要 region/mask/proxy，而不是整个 project；本地先压缩、裁剪、去除 EXIF；对隐私敏感素材提供 local-only 或 enterprise controls。对视频可以上传 proxy 或 selected segments。系统要透明告诉用户哪些数据会送云端。这个点很适合展示 user trust 思维。

Real-time Collaboration 与 Review Systems#

1. Creative review 系统怎么设计？#

需要 asset version、comment anchors、annotations、approval state、notifications、role permissions。视频评论要绑定 timestamp/range，图片评论要绑定 region/layer。Review decision 进入 audit log，影响发布权限。AI 生成资产可以自动附上 model/provenance summary，帮助 reviewer 判断。

2. 如何做 comment anchors？#

图片 anchor 可以是 normalized coordinates、layer id、asset version；视频 anchor 是 timeline timestamp、clip id、frame range；文档/design anchor 是 element id。必须绑定 asset version，否则后续编辑后评论位置会漂移。复杂场景下需要 anchor migration 或显示 “comment belongs to older version”。这是 collaboration 系统的细节亮点。

3. Review workflow 和 agent 如何结合？#

Agent 可以生成 draft、整理变更摘要、检查 brand compliance、回答 reviewer 问题，但 approval 应由 human 或 policy owner 完成。Agent 可以把 reviewer feedback 转成 edit tasks。所有自动操作都保存 trace。这样 agent 提高审阅效率，但不会绕过治理。

AI Media Incident Case Studies#

1. Case：生成任务大量 timeout#

定位路径：先看 queue backlog、GPU worker health、model latency、preprocess/transcode latency、dependency errors。分 media type 和 model version 看是否某个新模型导致。止血：暂停低优先级 batch、扩容 worker、回滚模型、降低 max duration/resolution。长期：capacity model、queue SLO、autoscaling、stage-level alert、load test。

2. Case：输出资产缺少 provenance metadata#

影响是 trust/compliance 受损。定位：检查 generation step 是否写 metadata、transcode/export 是否丢弃 metadata、CDN/format conversion 是否剥离 sidecar。修复：把 provenance 作为 required workflow step，export 后验证，失败则 block publish 或 attach sidecar。加 regression tests 覆盖常见 format。

3. Case：某企业客户看到另一个客户的素材#

这是严重 security incident。立即 revoke access、disable affected endpoint/cache、保留 logs、通知 security/legal、评估 blast radius。根因可能是 cache key 缺 tenant_id、search index filter 漏洞、signed URL 权限过宽。修复要包括 tenant isolation tests、cache key audit、permission enforcement in backend、postmortem 和客户通知流程。

4. Case：AI agent 删除或覆盖用户作品#

先恢复 asset version，禁用相关 destructive tool 或改成 approval-required。查 agent trace、tool permission、UI confirmation、operation log。修复：draft-by-default、non-destructive operations、risk-tier tool policy、undo guarantee、agent eval 添加 destructive scenario。对 creative 产品，用户信任比单次自动化效率更重要。

5. Case：video lip sync 质量在某语言下降#

定位：按 language、speaker type、duration、model version、audio quality 分段。看 ASR、translation、TTS、lip sync 各 stage 指标。可能是语言数据不足、phoneme alignment 问题、音频噪声、模型更新。修复：rollback 特定语言模型、加入 human review、更新 eval set。这个 case 展示 media pipeline 细分 debug 能力。

More Project Pitches You Can Use#

1. Project pitch：Creative AI Job Platform#

目标是统一 image/video/audio GenAI jobs。核心能力：job API、priority queue、GPU/CPU worker pools、artifact store、stage-level metrics、policy checks、content credentials、cost attribution。价值：新模型和新产品 surface 不用重建 pipeline，用户得到可查询、可取消、可恢复的生成体验。面试中可作为 infrastructure platform 项目讲。

2. Project pitch：Media Provenance Platform#

目标是把 Content Credentials/provenance 变成 first-class platform capability。每个生成/编辑/export 操作都产生 provenance event，签名后嵌入或 sidecar 存储。价值：增强用户信任、支持企业审计、降低合规风险。技术点：asset graph、key management、metadata preservation、verification API、privacy tiers。

3. Project pitch：Creative Agent Orchestrator#

目标是让 agent 安全调用 creative tools。核心组件：planner、tool registry、policy engine、workflow state、human approval、audit trace、artifact versioning。价值：把自然语言意图转成可编辑 creative outputs，同时控制 side effects。面试中强调 non-destructive by default、tool permissions、scenario eval。

4. Project pitch：Enterprise Brand Governance for GenAI#

目标是确保生成内容符合 brand 和 rights。系统接入 brand kit、templates、rights metadata、policy engine、review workflow。生成后做 brand compliance check，发布前做 rights check。价值：企业可以 scale content production，而不失控。技术点：metadata schema、policy-as-code、review state machine、analytics。

5. Project pitch：Media Observability and Cost Platform#

目标是把每个 generation job 的 stage latency、GPU/CPU cost、failure reason、quality signal、user acceptance 统一记录。价值：工程能定位瓶颈，产品能理解用户行为，财务能做 cost attribution。技术点：OpenTelemetry、structured events、warehouse model、dashboards、SLO alerts。

6. Implementation Deep Dive：把五个 project pitch 讲成系统设计#

这五个 project pitch 可以作为 adt / GenAI product systems 面试的备用弹药。每个都要准备 API contract、state model、critical path、failure mode 和 operational metric。

Creative AI Job Platform#

System design：统一 ImageJob、VideoJob、AudioJob、DocumentJob 到一个 AIJob abstraction。API 接收 operation_type、input_artifacts、quality_mode、tenant_policy、priority。Control plane 管 state machine；data plane 由 CPU preprocess workers、GPU model workers、postprocess/export workers 执行。

Implementation details：

  • State machine：created -> validated -> queued -> preprocessing -> running_model -> postprocessing -> safety_check -> completed / failed / canceled。
  • Queue：按 interactive、standard、batch、enterprise_reserved 分 priority queue。
  • Worker contract：worker 从 job store 拉 step，写 step result，不直接改最终 job state。
  • Idempotency：job_id + step_id + input_artifact_hash + worker_version 防重复产物。
  • Backpressure：GPU queue 超阈值时新 job 降级到 async 或提示 wait time。

Interview line：

The hard part is not invoking a model; it is making long-running creative work cancellable, observable, resumable, and safe to insert back into a user project.

Media Provenance Platform#

System design：把 provenance 作为 required platform step，而不是 export-time afterthought。所有生成、编辑、转码、导出操作都产生日志和 signed provenance record。Public metadata 用于用户/外部验证，private audit log 用于 enterprise support 和合规。

Implementation details：

  • ProvenanceEvent(event_id, artifact_id, parent_ids, operation, actor, model_version, tool_version, timestamp, policy_result)。
  • Export path 必须验证 provenance exists，否则 block publish 或 attach sidecar。
  • 对 PNG/JPEG/MP4/PDF 等格式分别支持 embedded metadata 和 sidecar fallback。
  • KMS 管签名 key，按 tenant/region 分 key policy。
  • Privacy tier：公开只展示 AI involvement 和 basic lineage，内部 audit 才能看完整 prompt/source。

Failure modes：转码剥离 metadata、CDN 缓存旧 sidecar、derived asset 权限继承错误、跨 tenant provenance lookup。每个都要有 regression test。

Creative Agent Orchestrator#

System design：agent 不直接操作资产，而是通过 tool registry 和 workflow engine 提交可审计 operation。Planner 生成 plan，policy engine 检查 tool permission，workflow 执行 step，human approval 处理 destructive/high-risk operation。

Implementation details：

  • Tool schema：name、input_schema、output_schema、required_scopes、risk_level、cost_class、latency_class。
  • Memory/RAG：只检索用户有权限的 project/brand/asset context。
  • Action policy：read-only tools 可自动执行；edit tools 生成 draft；delete/publish/billing tools require approval。
  • Audit trace：每个 tool call 记录 reason、input reference、output artifact、policy decision、model version。
  • Eval：scenario-based tests，覆盖 wrong asset、permission denial、destructive instruction、ambiguous request。

Standout answer：

I would make the agent non-destructive by default. It can propose and draft, but irreversible operations require explicit policy and approval.

Enterprise Brand Governance for GenAI#

System design：把 brand kit、rights metadata、policy rules、review workflow 接到 generation 和 publish path。模型可以生成内容，但平台决定内容是否可用于某个 channel / region / customer campaign。

Implementation details：

  • Brand kit schema：logo、color palette、font、tone、disclaimer、approved templates、restricted phrases。
  • Rights schema：territory、expiration、channel、talent/music/license constraints。
  • Validator：generation 后检查 visual/text/audio brand compliance，输出 allow / fix / review / deny。
  • Review workflow：low-confidence 或 high-risk output 进入 reviewer queue；review decision 写 audit log。
  • Policy-as-code：规则版本化，支持 per-tenant override 和 dry-run。

Metrics：brand violation rate、review queue time、auto-fix success、publish block reason、customer override rate。

Media Observability and Cost Platform#

System design：把 AI job 的 product trace、model trace、infra trace、cost trace 统一。目标不是多做 dashboard，而是让一次用户投诉能从 project/job 一路查到 model version、artifact lineage、GPU worker、policy decision 和 cost。

Implementation details：

  • Trace dimensions：tenant、project、job、artifact、model_version、worker_version、route_policy、request_class。
  • Metrics：low-cardinality SLO metrics 进 Prometheus；high-cardinality event 进 warehouse/log lake。
  • Cost model：CPU seconds、GPU seconds、storage bytes、egress、third-party model cost、retry waste。
  • Dashboard：按 product surface、model、tenant、media type、failure code、cost driver 分解。
  • Daily ops：top regressions、top cost anomalies、top DLQ reasons、p95/p99 by stage。

Interview line：

Observability should connect customer impact, model behavior, workflow stage, infrastructure health, and unit economics in one debug path.

ByteDance / AIBrix-style LLM Inference Infrastructure Prep#
