class IndexedChunk(BaseModel):
    chunk_id: str
    asset_id: str
    tenant_id: str
    modality: str
    embedding_version: str
    index_version: str
    acl_hash: str
    text: Optional[str]
    artifact_ref: Optional[str]

痛点：

delete propagation 必须可验证。 · embedding model 升级需要双写/双读或 shadow index。 · ACL filter 不能只靠客户端。 · index freshness lag 要进入 SLO。 · vector search 结果要能解释，否则 RAG 质量问题很难 debug。

7. Workflow implementation：async、retry、idempotency 怎么做#

长任务不要让 HTTP request 一直挂着。典型模式：

text
POST /workflows
  -> returns workflow_id

GET /workflows/{workflow_id}
  -> status, progress, artifact refs

POST /workflows/{workflow_id}/cancel
  -> cooperative cancellation

Workflow state machine：

text
CREATED
  -> PLANNED
  -> RETRIEVING
  -> GENERATING
  -> WRITING_ARTIFACT
  -> COMPLETED
  -> FAILED / CANCELLED / NEEDS_APPROVAL

关键实现原则：

每个 step 有 idempotency_key，retry 不产生重复 artifact。 · external side effect，比如发送邮件、写生产项目、执行工具，要有 confirmation 或 compensation。 · worker 只拿 lease，不能永久占有任务。 · task timeout 和 heartbeat 区分，避免 silent stuck。 · partial output 可以保存，但必须标明 incomplete。

Kafka event 例子：

json
{
  "event_type": "workflow.step.completed",
  "workflow_id": "wf_123",
  "step_id": "rerank_2",
  "tenant_id": "t_456",
  "model_version": "reranker_2026_06",
  "latency_ms": 84,
  "artifact_ref": "s3://artifacts/wf_123/rerank.json"
}

8. Observability implementation：trace 怎么把系统串起来#

OpenTelemetry span 设计：

text
ai.request
  -> gateway.auth
  -> planner.create_plan
  -> retrieval.embed_query
  -> retrieval.ann_search
  -> retrieval.rerank
  -> llm.generate
  -> tool.execute
  -> artifact.write

关键 attributes：

python
span.set_attribute("tenant_id_hash", hash_tenant(req.tenant_id))
span.set_attribute("workflow_id", workflow_id)
span.set_attribute("model.name", route.model_name)
span.set_attribute("model.version", route.model_version)
span.set_attribute("ai.input_tokens", input_tokens)
span.set_attribute("ai.output_tokens", output_tokens)
span.set_attribute("ai.queue_wait_ms", queue_wait_ms)
span.set_attribute("ai.cost_usd", estimated_cost)
span.set_attribute("retrieval.index_version", index_version)

注意：

不直接记录 raw prompt、raw media、PII。 · 高价值 debug 可以做 redacted prompt snapshot。 · trace id 写入 Kafka event、workflow DB、model logs，方便跨系统 join。 · eval event 和 serving trace 关联，才能解释 quality regression。

9. Deployment implementation：Kubernetes 上怎么落#

Kubernetes 资源分层：

text
Namespace per environment / tenant class
  -> API/control services on CPU node pool
  -> Ray cluster on mixed CPU/GPU node pool
  -> vLLM deployments on GPU node pool
  -> Triton deployments on GPU node pool
  -> index/query services on memory-optimized node pool

关键对象：

Deployment：gateway、control plane、router。 · RayCluster：Ray head/worker lifecycle。 · HorizontalPodAutoscaler / custom autoscaler：CPU service 扩缩容。 · KEDA：按 queue lag/event backlog 扩容。 · NVIDIA device plugin：GPU resource expose。 · Node pool + taints/tolerations：隔离 A10/L4/H100 等不同 GPU。 · ConfigMap/Secret：模型 endpoint、policy、credentials。 · ServiceMonitor：Prometheus scrape。

GPU serving autoscaling 不要只看 CPU：

vLLM：pending tokens、TTFT、TPOT、KV cache usage、queue wait。 · Triton：queue duration、inference duration、batch size、GPU memory。 · Ray：pending tasks、actor backlog、object store pressure。 · Vector DB：query p99、segment load、cache hit、shard pressure。

10. Implementation tradeoffs：你要能 defending decisions#

Decision为什么这么选风险Mitigation
Ray Serve + vLLMRay 做 composition，vLLM 做 token engine双层调度复杂明确 ownership，统一 tracing
Triton for embedding/rerankbatching 成熟，适合 encoder workloadPython backend 可能慢核心模型用 TensorRT/ONNX backend
Kafka for eventsreplay、audit、backfill运维成本高schema registry、retention、DLQ
Vector + lexical hybridsemantic + exact keyword/filterranking 融合复杂offline eval + online A/B
Durable workflow state支持 retry/cancel/audit实现复杂标准 state machine/idempotency
Engine abstraction可替换 vLLM/SGLang/TensorRT抽象过度只抽象 metrics/routing/health/input contract
Artifact references避免大对象复制需要 artifact lifecycleTTL、ownership、lineage、GC

一句话：

I would not optimize for a pretty architecture diagram. I would optimize for replaceable engines, durable state, observable requests, controlled cost, and product teams being able to ship new AI workflows without rebuilding the platform.

11. Interview project walkthrough：2 分钟版本#

可以这样讲：

I built or would design a multimodal AI execution platform with a clear separation between product workflow and model execution. The client sends bounded context and artifact references to an API gateway. The control plane resolves tenant policy, model versions, quota, and routing rules. A workflow layer stores durable state for long-running tasks, while Ray handles distributed execution and service composition.

For model serving, I use Triton for encoder-style workloads like image/text/audio embeddings, reranking, safety classifiers, and media analysis. I use vLLM or SGLang for LLM generation and agent-style structured generation. Retrieval uses a hybrid vector and lexical index with ACL filtering and index versioning. Events flow through Kafka so indexing, telemetry, feedback, and evaluation are replayable.

The main tradeoff is keeping the system flexible without making it vague. I keep a small engine abstraction around routing, health, metrics, and input/output contracts, but I do not hide engine-specific strengths like vLLM KV cache metrics or Triton dynamic batching. Operationally, I watch TTFT, TPOT, queue wait, KV cache pressure, embedding throughput, index freshness, workflow retry rate, quality metrics, and cost per successful output.

12. Role qualification mapping：把 JD 要求落到实现细节#

这一段可以帮助你在 ByteDance/AIBrix、Core Infra、Inference Platform、AI Platform 面试中把 JD 的每个要求映射到项目实现。不要说“我了解 Kubernetes/Ray/vLLM”，要说你在系统里具体负责哪一层、做了什么决策、看什么指标、怎么处理故障。

Qualification mapping：

  • Large model inference：项目里体现为 vLLM/SGLang/TensorRT-LLM serving、KV cache、prefill/decode、token-aware routing。面试表达：I reason about LLM serving in tokens, KV cache, TTFT/TPOT, not only QPS.
  • Distributed systems：项目里体现为 Ray actors/tasks、Kafka replay、workflow state、multi-replica routing。面试表达：I separate durable state, compute workers, event log, and model engine.
  • High-performance networking：项目里体现为 NCCL/topology、streaming response、gRPC/Triton、cross-zone traffic control。面试表达：I avoid moving raw media/tensors across services and optimize locality.
  • Resource management：项目里体现为 GPU pool、quota、model placement、node pool、autoscaling。面试表达：Scheduling is based on memory, topology, SLO, and token demand.
  • Request routing：项目里体现为 model router、tenant policy、engine choice、fallback。面试表达：Routing is policy-driven and observable, not hard-coded if-else.
  • Monitoring：项目里体现为 OpenTelemetry、Prometheus、Grafana、engine metrics、quality events。面试表达：I monitor latency, cost, quality, retrieval freshness, and safety.
  • Orchestration：项目里体现为 Kubernetes、KubeRay、Ray Serve、workflow engine。面试表达：Kubernetes manages resources; Ray manages distributed execution; workflow state is durable.
  • Docker/Kubernetes：项目里体现为 image build、GPU device plugin、CRD/controller、HPA/KEDA、taints/tolerations。面试表达：I can explain both container packaging and cluster scheduling behavior.
  • Programming：项目里体现为 Python for ML/workflow、Go for controllers、C++/CUDA for perf-sensitive kernels。面试表达：I choose language by layer: Python for model glue, Go for control plane, C++/CUDA for hot path.

13. Large model inference implementation：从 request 到 GPU kernel#

面试官问 large model inference，不要只说“用 vLLM”。可以按 execution path 讲：

text
HTTP/gRPC request
  -> tokenizer
  -> request admission
  -> scheduler queue
  -> prefill batch
  -> KV cache allocation
  -> decode loop
  -> sampling
  -> detokenization
  -> streaming response
  -> metrics/accounting

关键设计点：

  • Admission control：根据 prompt tokens、expected output tokens、tenant quota、KV cache pressure 决定是否接收。
  • Prefill/decode separation：prefill compute-heavy，decode memory-bandwidth-heavy，两者混合会影响 TTFT/TPOT。
  • KV cache manager：显存不是只放 weights，还要放每个 sequence 的 KV cache。
  • Batching policy：max_num_batched_tokens、max_num_seqs、chunked prefill 影响吞吐和 tail latency。
  • Sampling：temperature、top-p、top-k、beam/speculative decoding 会影响质量和延迟。
  • Streaming：response streaming 降低 perceived latency，但要处理 cancellation、partial output、cost accounting。

critical pseudo-code：

python
def admit_request(req, quota, engine_state):
    prompt_tokens = estimate_tokens(req.prompt)
    expected_total = prompt_tokens + req.max_output_tokens
    if quota.remaining_tokens < expected_total:
        return Reject("quota_exceeded")
    if engine_state.kv_free_blocks < estimate_kv_blocks(expected_total):
        return QueueOrFallback("kv_pressure")
    return Admit(priority=req.priority, estimated_tokens=expected_total)

高级点：

For LLM inference, the serving unit is not request count. It is prompt tokens, decode tokens, KV cache blocks, queue wait, and SLO-compliant useful output. A scheduler that only sees QPS will make poor decisions under long-context workloads.

14. Distributed and parallel systems：Ray/Kubernetes/engine 三层调度#

AI infra 里常见问题是三层 scheduler 同时存在：

text
Kubernetes scheduler
  -> places pods on nodes

Ray scheduler
  -> places tasks/actors on cluster resources

vLLM/SGLang scheduler
  -> places sequences into token batches

每层 ownership：

Scheduler看什么资源决策粒度典型问题
Kubernetespod CPU/GPU/memory、node labels、taintspod placementGPU topology 不够语义化
Raytask/actor resources、placement groupPython task/actorobject store pressure、actor recovery
vLLM/SGLangtokens、KV cache、sequence statetoken batchlong context、tail latency

implementation principle：

Kubernetes 负责 node/pod isolation，例如 GPU type、region、tenant class。 · Ray 负责 distributed execution，例如 batch embedding、eval fan-out、workflow worker。 · vLLM/SGLang 负责 token execution，例如 continuous batching、KV cache、decode loop。 · control plane 做跨层决策，例如 route request 到哪个 model pool、是否 fallback、是否 shed load。

调度 scoring 示例：

python
def score_replica(replica, req):
    return (
        0.35 * normalize(replica.kv_free_ratio)
        + 0.25 * normalize(1 / (1 + replica.queue_wait_ms))
        + 0.20 * cache_locality(replica, req.prefix_hash)
        + 0.10 * region_affinity(replica, req.region)
        + 0.10 * cost_score(replica.gpu_type)
    )

面试里可以补：

I try to avoid hidden scheduling conflicts. If Kubernetes, Ray, and vLLM all make independent decisions without shared metrics, the system looks scalable on paper but behaves unpredictably under load.

15. High-performance networking：不要让 network 成为隐形瓶颈#

AI infra 的 network bottleneck 主要来自三类：

  1. Model parallel communication：tensor parallel / pipeline parallel 依赖 NCCL、NVLink、InfiniBand/RDMA、EFA。
  2. Data movement：raw media、embedding vectors、feature tensors、artifact transfer。
  3. Serving traffic：streaming tokens、retrieval fan-out、cross-region routing。

设计原则：

大 media 不在微服务之间复制，传 artifact_ref。 · 同一模型的 tensor-parallel replicas 尽量放在 topology-friendly GPU 上。 · embedding/reranking 尽量 batch，减少 small request overhead。 · cross-region 只传必要 metadata，避免 raw customer data 跨区。 · request router 做 region affinity 和 cache locality。 · streaming response 要避免每个 token 都产生过重 logging/flush。

可以提到的技术：

NCCL：GPU collective communication。 · RDMA / InfiniBand / AWS EFA：高性能跨机通信。 · gRPC：Triton/generic internal service 通信。 · HTTP streaming / SSE / WebSocket：LLM token streaming。 · zero-copy / mmap / pinned memory：低层优化概念。

面试表达：

For multimodal AI workloads, network optimization starts with not moving data unnecessarily. I pass artifact references, keep compute near data when possible, and only optimize lower-level networking after I understand whether the bottleneck is GPU collective communication, media transfer, or serving fan-out.

16. Kubernetes / Docker implementation：从 image 到 GPU pool#

Docker image 设计：

dockerfile
FROM nvidia/cuda:12.4.1-runtime-ubuntu22.04
WORKDIR /app
COPY requirements.txt .
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r requirements.txt
COPY . .
CMD ["python", "-m", "ai_platform.router"]

生产注意点：

CUDA、driver、PyTorch、vLLM/Triton 版本要 compatibility matrix。 · image 分层要避免每次模型变更都 rebuild 巨大镜像。 · 模型权重可以用 init container、sidecar cache、PVC、object store lazy loading。 · security：non-root user、read-only filesystem、SBOM、image scan。

Kubernetes GPU deployment 关键字段：

yaml
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: vllm-llama-serving
spec:
  replicas: 4
  template:
    spec:
      nodeSelector:
        accelerator: nvidia-h100
      tolerations:
        - key: gpu
          operator: Exists
      containers:
        - name: vllm
          image: ai/vllm-serving:prod
          resources:
            limits:
              nvidia.com/gpu: 1
          env:
            - name: MODEL_ID
              value: llama-family-prod

Kubernetes operator/controller 能展示更强 infra 深度。可以这样解释：

go
// Reconcile desired ModelDeployment state into Kubernetes resources.
func (r *ModelDeploymentReconciler) Reconcile(ctx context.Context, req ctrl.Request) (ctrl.Result, error) {
    md := &inferencev1.ModelDeployment{}
    if err := r.Get(ctx, req.NamespacedName, md); err != nil {
        return ctrl.Result{}, client.IgnoreNotFound(err)
    }
    // 1. validate model/engine/resource profile
    // 2. create or update Deployment/Service/HPA/ServiceMonitor
    // 3. update status: Ready, Warming, Degraded, Failed
    // 4. requeue if model is still warming or metrics are missing
    return ctrl.Result{RequeueAfter: time.Minute}, nil
}

面试要点：

controller 必须 idempotent。 · status update 要 rate-limited，避免 API server 压力。 · finalizer 用于清理 model cache、routing entry、metrics。 · readiness 不等于 pod running，模型必须 loaded/warmed。

17. GPU orchestration and isolation：资源管理怎么做#

GPU orchestration 的难点不是“申请一张 GPU”，而是如何在多 tenant、多 model、多 SLO 下提高利用率又不互相影响。

资源维度：

GPU type：L4/A10/A100/H100，成本和性能不同。 · GPU memory：weights + KV cache + activation + adapter。 · Topology：single GPU、NVLink、PCIe、multi-node。 · MIG：隔离好，但对大模型和灵活调度有限制。 · Node pool：interactive、batch、training、embedding 分池。 · Priority/preemption：batch 可抢占，interactive 尽量不可抢占。 · Quota：tenant/model/team 级别。

隔离策略：

RiskSolution
noisy neighbortenant quota、priority queue、separate pool
data leakagenamespace/IAM/secret isolation、encrypted artifact、ACL
GPU OOMadmission control、KV budget、max context、load shedding
hot modelwarm pool、replica autoscale、prefix cache
low utilizationcontinuous batching、dynamic batching、pooling、batch jobs fill idle

capacity planning：

text
required_gpus =
  peak_output_tokens_per_sec / measured_tokens_per_sec_per_gpu
  * headroom_factor
  * redundancy_factor

但真实生产还要加：

prompt token distribution。 · output length distribution。 · long-tail tenant spike。 · model warmup time。 · region failover。 · batch workload opportunistic scheduling。

18. Cloud ML platform mapping：AWS/Azure/GCP 怎么讲#

你可以把 cloud provider 讲成可替换 substrate，而不是绑定一家。

NeedAWSAzureGCP自建/K8s
Managed trainingSageMaker TrainingAzure ML JobsVertex AI TrainingKubeflow/Ray/Argo
Managed inferenceSageMaker EndpointAzure ML Online EndpointVertex AI EndpointKServe/Ray Serve/vLLM
Object storeS3Blob StorageGCSMinIO/Ceph
StreamingMSK/KinesisEvent HubsPub/SubKafka/Pulsar
IAM/secretsIAM/KMS/Secrets ManagerEntra/Key VaultIAM/KMS/Secret ManagerVault/External Secrets
GPU computeEC2 GPU/EKSNC/ND VM/AKSA3/GKEbare metal K8s

面试表达：

I design the platform so cloud-specific services are behind clear interfaces: object store, secret store, event log, GPU node pool, model endpoint. On AWS I might use EKS, S3, IAM, KMS, MSK, and SageMaker where managed lifecycle helps. But the core contracts should still work on Kubernetes with Ray/vLLM/Triton.

19. ML systems depth：PyTorch、DeepSpeed、Ray、vLLM 怎么连接#

训练和推理的技术栈不同，但要讲清楚 lifecycle：

text
PyTorch training
  -> DeepSpeed/FSDP distributed training
  -> checkpoint conversion
  -> eval and safety test
  -> model registry
  -> quantization/engine build if needed
  -> vLLM/SGLang/Triton deployment
  -> canary
  -> online monitoring

关键知识点：

  • PyTorch DDP/FSDP：数据并行、参数/梯度/optimizer state sharding。
  • DeepSpeed ZeRO：optimizer/gradient/parameter 分片，降低训练显存。
  • tensor parallel：单层矩阵切分，多 GPU 推理/训练。
  • pipeline parallel：层切分，micro-batch 流水。
  • sequence parallel/context parallel：长上下文切分。
  • checkpoint conversion：训练 checkpoint 不一定能直接 serving，需要 weight format、quantization、tokenizer、config 对齐。
  • quantization：INT8/FP8/AWQ/GPTQ，性能和质量 tradeoff。

面试表达：

I connect training and inference through explicit artifacts: data snapshot, code version, checkpoint, tokenizer, eval report, model card, quantization profile, serving config, and rollback policy. Without that lifecycle, ML infra becomes a collection of scripts.

20. Programming language positioning：Python / Go / C++ / CUDA 怎么说#

不要泛泛说“我会 Python”。按 layer 讲语言选择：

LayerLanguage为什么
ML workflow / serving gluePythonPyTorch、Ray、FastAPI、Triton client、eval ecosystem
Control plane / controllerGoKubernetes client-go、controller-runtime、high-concurrency service
Performance hot pathC++low-latency serving、custom operators、native extension
GPU kernelCUDA/Triton languageattention、sampling、pre/post-processing kernel
Infra toolsPython/GoCLI、automation、load test、debug tools

Python critical libraries：

  • ray, ray[serve], torch, transformers, vllm, tritonclient, fastapi, pydantic, opentelemetry-sdk, prometheus-client, kubernetes。

Go critical libraries：

controller-runtime, client-go, zap, prometheus/client_golang, grpc-go。

C++/CUDA talking points：

理解 GPU kernel launch overhead、memory coalescing、shared memory、warp divergence。 · 理解 attention kernel、KV cache layout、quantized matmul、NCCL collective。 · 不需要声称手写了所有 kernel，但要能和 kernel/runtime 团队沟通 bottleneck。

一句话：

My strongest implementation layer is Python and distributed AI systems, but I understand where Go control-plane code and C++/CUDA hot-path optimization fit into the production stack.

21. Open-source and cross-functional operating model#

这个 JD 强调 open-source innovation 和 global cross-functional collaboration。你可以这样讲：

对开源项目先做 issue reproduction、benchmark、minimal patch、upstream discussion。 · 贡献前先理解 maintainer design philosophy，不做只适合内部 fork 的 patch。 · 内部平台保持 engine adapter，避免深度 fork vLLM/SGLang/Ray 后无法升级。 · 和 research team 对齐 model capability、checkpoint format、eval requirements。 · 和 product team 对齐 latency、quality、user workflow、failure UX。 · 和 security/legal 对齐 tenant isolation、data retention、audit、model license。 · 和 SRE/infra 对齐 SLO、oncall、capacity、incident response。

面试表达：

I try to turn cross-functional ambiguity into contracts: model contract, data contract, serving SLO, rollout gate, and incident ownership. That is how an AI platform stays fast without becoming fragile.

22. Implementation Interview Q&A Bank：覆盖 JD 高频追问#

这一节用于临场练习。回答时要遵循一个顺序：先说 principle，再说 architecture，再说 implementation detail，最后说 tradeoff / metric。这样面试官会感觉你不是背工具，而是真的做过或能 owner 这类系统。

A. Large model inference#

Q1: 你怎么解释 LLM inference 的主要瓶颈？

强回答：

LLM inference has two very different phases: prefill and decode. Prefill processes the prompt in parallel and is usually compute-heavy. Decode generates one token at a time and is often memory-bandwidth and KV-cache limited. So I would not optimize only for QPS. I look at prompt tokens/sec, output tokens/sec, TTFT, TPOT, queue wait, KV cache pressure, and cost per useful token.

补充点：

TTFT 主要受 queue wait、tokenization、prefill、prefix cache 命中影响。 · TPOT 主要受 decode step、batching、memory bandwidth、NCCL 通信影响。 · long context 会让 KV cache 成本放大。 · streaming 可以改善 perceived latency，但不会减少 GPU compute。

常见陷阱：

只说 GPU utilization。 · 把 batch size 越大越好当成绝对真理。 · 不区分 prefill 和 decode。

Q2: vLLM 为什么能提高吞吐？

强回答：

vLLM improves serving efficiency mainly through PagedAttention and continuous batching. PagedAttention manages KV cache in blocks, reducing memory fragmentation for mixed-length requests. Continuous batching allows new requests to join between decoding steps, so GPU work is not blocked by the slowest request in a static batch.

可以继续展开：

KV cache block table 类似 virtual memory 思路。 · max_num_batched_tokens 控制一个 step 能处理多少 token。 · max_num_seqs 控制并发 sequence 数。 · prefix cache 适合相同 system prompt、RAG template、agent instruction。

Q3: 如果 p99 latency 突然升高，你怎么排查 LLM serving？

排查顺序：

  1. Traffic shape：prompt length、output length、tenant/model 是否变化。
  2. Queue：gateway queue、router queue、vLLM waiting queue、Ray actor backlog。
  3. KV cache：free blocks、eviction、OOM、long-context request 占比。
  4. Prefill/decode：TTFT 变差还是 TPOT 变差。
  5. GPU：SM utilization、HBM bandwidth、NCCL、GPU memory。
  6. CPU/network：tokenizer、detokenizer、JSON streaming、TLS、logging。
  7. Recent changes：model version、prompt version、driver image、autoscaler policy。

一句话：

I always split latency into queue wait, prefill, decode, retrieval/tool time, and postprocess. Otherwise average latency hides the real bottleneck.

Q4: TensorRT-LLM 什么时候值得用？

强回答：

I would consider TensorRT-LLM when the endpoint is high-volume, stable, NVIDIA-centric, and cost-sensitive enough to justify engine build and deployment complexity. For fast-moving product experiments, vLLM or SGLang usually gives better iteration speed. For mature hot paths, TensorRT-LLM can improve latency and throughput, but it requires stronger build pipeline, compatibility tests, and rollback discipline.

B. Distributed and parallel systems#

Q5: 你怎么理解 distributed systems 在 AI infra 里的特殊性？

强回答：

AI infra combines normal distributed-system problems with model-specific resource constraints. We still need retries, idempotency, leader/follower thinking, backpressure, durable state, and observability. But the resources are not just CPU and memory. We also schedule GPU memory, KV cache, topology, model warmup, token demand, and data locality.

关键点：

workflow state 和 worker compute 分离。 · event log 用于 replay/backfill/audit。 · model workers 是 expensive and stateful。 · failure 不只包括 5xx，还包括 quality regression、cost spike、stale index。

Q6: Ray 和 Kubernetes 分别解决什么？

强回答：

Kubernetes is the container orchestration and isolation substrate. It schedules pods, manages services, RBAC, node pools, deployments, and resource limits. Ray is the distributed execution runtime inside that substrate. It schedules Python tasks and actors, manages object refs, placement groups, and distributed compute graphs. I use Kubernetes for cluster/resource lifecycle and Ray for dynamic ML execution.

追问回答：

KubeRay 把 RayCluster 作为 Kubernetes CRD 管。 · Kubernetes 不理解 Ray object store 和 actor lifecycle。 · Ray 不应该替代 IAM、network policy、durable DB、tenant governance。

Q7: 三层调度怎么避免互相打架？

强回答：

I make each scheduler own a different decision level. Kubernetes owns pod placement and isolation. Ray owns task/actor placement and distributed execution. vLLM/SGLang owns token batching and KV cache. The platform control plane collects metrics from all three and makes higher-level routing decisions.

落地方式：

K8s labels/taints 区分 GPU type、tenant class、region。 · Ray placement groups 管多 GPU actor。 · vLLM metrics 暴露 queue wait、KV usage、tokens/sec。 · router 用 shared metrics 做 replica scoring。

C. High-performance networking#

Q8: AI infra 中 network bottleneck 常见在哪里？

强回答：

I look at three networking paths: GPU-to-GPU communication for distributed training/inference, service-to-service traffic in inference workflows, and data/artifact movement for multimodal workloads. The first involves NCCL, NVLink, InfiniBand, RDMA, or AWS EFA. The second involves gRPC/HTTP streaming and fan-out. The third is often solved by not moving raw media, but passing artifact references.

关键点：

tensor parallel 需要高带宽低延迟通信。 · pipeline parallel 要考虑 bubble 和 micro-batch。 · retrieval fan-out 容易造成 tail latency。 · raw video/audio/image 跨服务复制会压垮 network。

Q9: 你会如何减少跨服务数据移动？

强回答：

I would use a content-addressed artifact store and pass references instead of payloads. Services exchange asset_id, artifact_ref, embedding_version, and workflow_id. Heavy media stays in object storage or cache near compute. Only lightweight metadata and derived tensors move through request paths.

可补充：

signed URL / scoped token 控制访问。 · artifact lifecycle 需要 TTL、lineage、GC。 · 大 embedding batch 走 batch pipeline，不走同步 API。

D. Resource management / scheduling / routing#

Q10: 你怎么设计 GPU-aware scheduler？

强回答：

I would not schedule only by GPU count. I would include model memory footprint, KV cache budget, GPU type, topology, queue wait, tenant priority, region, cache locality, and SLO class. Interactive traffic and batch traffic should share capacity carefully: batch can fill idle GPUs but must be preemptible or throttled.

调度信号：

model weight memory。 · expected prompt/output tokens。 · KV free blocks。 · warm/cold model status。 · pending tokens。 · p95 TTFT/TPOT。 · node GPU type and topology。

Q11: request routing 你怎么做？

强回答：

I use policy-driven routing. The router checks tenant policy, model capability, privacy class, region, current engine metrics, and fallback rules. The route decision is written to trace so we can explain why a request went to vLLM, SGLang, Triton, a smaller model, or async mode.

常见策略：

capability routing：vision/tool/long-context/JSON mode。 · SLO routing：interactive vs async vs batch。 · cost routing：small model first，fallback large model。 · locality routing：prefix cache、region、data locality。 · safety routing：restricted tenant/model allowlist。

Q12: autoscaling LLM inference 为什么难？

强回答：

Autoscaling is hard because demand is token-shaped, not request-shaped. GPU nodes are slow to provision, models are slow to load, and KV cache pressure can increase before QPS changes. I would scale on pending tokens, queue wait, TTFT/TPOT, KV pressure, and model warmup time, not just CPU or request count.

补充：

cold start 包括 image pull、weight download、engine init、warmup。 · scale down 要避免杀掉有 active sequence 的 worker。 · 可以保留 warm pool。

E. Monitoring and orchestration#

Q13: AI infra monitoring 和普通 service monitoring 有什么不同？

强回答：

For normal services, latency, error rate, and saturation are the starting point. For AI systems, I also monitor token cost, model/version, prompt/version, retrieval freshness, index version, safety policy decisions, tool-call failures, output quality, and user feedback. A system can be available but still produce bad or expensive answers.

必备 dashboard：

Serving：TTFT、TPOT、tokens/sec、queue wait、KV usage。 · GPU：util、HBM、OOM、NCCL error。 · Retrieval：recall proxy、zero result、index freshness、reranker latency。 · Workflow：stuck jobs、retry、cancel、artifact failure。 · Quality：accept rate、eval score、judge fail。 · Cost：cost per successful request、GPU idle。

Q14: OpenTelemetry 在 AI workflow 里怎么用？

强回答：

I would create one trace per product request or workflow, with spans for auth, planning, retrieval, reranking, generation, tool execution, and artifact write. I attach safe metadata like model version, token counts, route decision, index version, and cost estimate, but I do not log raw prompts or customer media by default.

关键 attributes：

request_id / workflow_id。 · model_name / model_version。 · prompt_version / embedding_version / index_version。 · input_tokens / output_tokens。 · queue_wait_ms / engine / replica。 · policy_result / fallback_reason。

F. Docker and Kubernetes#

Q15: 你怎么把 inference service 部署到 Kubernetes？

强回答：

I separate CPU control services and GPU serving services into different node pools. The deployment declares GPU resource limits through the NVIDIA device plugin, uses node selectors or affinity for GPU type, has readiness probes that check model loaded and warmed, exports Prometheus metrics, and uses canary rollout with rollback. For Ray, I would use KubeRay to manage RayCluster lifecycle.

追问点：

readiness 不等于 container running。 · model loading 要有 warmup。 · GPU pods 要 tolerations/nodeSelector。 · secrets/IAM 不放 image。 · model weights 不一定打进 image。

Q16: Kubernetes controller 你怎么设计？

强回答：

A controller reconciles desired state to actual state. For a ModelDeployment CRD, the desired state includes model, engine, resource profile, replicas, rollout policy, and routing policy. The controller creates Deployments, Services, HPAs, ServiceMonitors, and routing entries, then updates status such as Warming, Ready, Degraded, or Failed.

原则：

reconcile 必须 idempotent。 · status update 要限流。 · finalizer 清理 model cache/routing entries。 · controller 不应在 reconcile loop 做长时间阻塞任务。 · 所有外部调用要 timeout/retry。

G. Programming language depth#

Q17: 这个岗位要求 Go/Rust/Python/C++，你怎么定位自己的语言能力？

强回答：

I use Python for ML workflow, serving glue, Ray, PyTorch, FastAPI, and evaluation because the ecosystem is strongest there. I use Go-style control-plane design for Kubernetes controllers, reconciliation, high-concurrency services, and typed APIs. For C++/CUDA, I understand where hot-path optimization matters: attention kernels, KV cache layout, quantized matmul, memory coalescing, and NCCL communication. I do not claim every layer should be in one language.

如果被追问 Go：

controller-runtime、client-go、CRD、Reconcile、Informer、WorkQueue。 · context cancellation、timeout、rate limit、structured logging。

如果被追问 Python：

asyncio、FastAPI、Pydantic、Ray、PyTorch、vLLM client、Triton client、OpenTelemetry。 · 注意 GIL、serialization、multiprocessing、async I/O。

H. Kubernetes/Ray cluster operations#

Q18: 你怎么 operate large-scale Ray cluster？

强回答：

I watch Ray task backlog, actor health, object store memory, spilling, node failure, placement group pending time, and autoscaler lag. For ML workloads, I avoid passing huge media through object store and prefer artifact references. I also separate interactive serving Ray clusters from batch processing clusters when SLO isolation matters.

常见故障：

object store OOM。 · actor restart 导致状态丢失。 · placement group pending。 · autoscaler 加节点慢。 · task 粒度太小导致 scheduler overhead。

Q19: 你怎么做 workload isolation？

强回答：

I isolate by tenant, workload class, GPU type, and risk level. Interactive workloads get priority queues and protected capacity. Batch jobs can be preemptible. Sensitive tenants may use separate namespaces, node pools, IAM roles, and encryption keys. At the model layer, I enforce model allowlists, quota, and request-level policy.

I. CUDA / inference engine#

Q20: 你对 CUDA 或 GPU programming 的理解到什么程度？

强回答：

My main experience is at ML systems and inference-engine integration level, but I understand the performance concepts needed to work with CUDA/runtime teams: kernel launch overhead, memory coalescing, shared memory, warp divergence, tensor cores, quantized matmul, attention kernels, KV cache layout, and NCCL collectives.

可以补：

如果 bottleneck 是 Python overhead，用 batching/async/compiled backend。 · 如果 bottleneck 是 HBM bandwidth，看 KV cache、quantization、attention kernel。 · 如果 bottleneck 是 multi-GPU communication，看 topology/NCCL。

Q21: vLLM、SGLang、TensorRT-LLM 你怎么选？

强回答：

vLLM is my default for general LLM serving because it balances performance and iteration speed. SGLang is attractive for structured generation, agent/RAG programs, and prefix reuse. TensorRT-LLM is for mature high-volume NVIDIA endpoints where performance per GPU justifies engine build complexity.

J. Cloud ML platforms#

Q22: AWS SageMaker / Azure ML / Vertex AI 和自建 Kubernetes 怎么取舍？

强回答：

Managed ML platforms are useful for fast lifecycle management, compliance defaults, and team productivity. Self-managed Kubernetes gives more control over custom engines, routing, GPU scheduling, and cost optimization. I would use managed services where they reduce undifferentiated work, but keep core contracts portable: model registry, artifact store, event log, endpoint contract, and observability.

场景：

  • SageMaker/Vertex/Azure ML：标准 training job、managed endpoint、experiment lifecycle。
  • EKS/GKE/AKS + Ray/vLLM/Triton：自定义 engine、multi-tenant routing、GPU-aware control plane。
  • 混合：managed training + self-managed inference，或 self-managed platform + cloud IAM/object store。

K. PyTorch / DeepSpeed / distributed training-inference lifecycle#

Q23: training system 和 inference system 怎么衔接？

强回答：

I connect them through explicit artifacts and gates: data snapshot, code commit, checkpoint, tokenizer, model config, eval report, safety report, quantization profile, serving config, canary result, and rollback policy. Training produces a checkpoint; production needs a deployable, evaluated, observable model artifact.

关键点：

PyTorch DDP/FSDP/DeepSpeed checkpoint format 可能需要 conversion。 · tokenizer/version 必须和 serving 对齐。 · quantization 后要重新 eval。 · LoRA adapter serving 要管理 base model compatibility。 · canary 不只看 latency，也看 quality/safety/cost。

Q24: DeepSpeed ZeRO、FSDP、Tensor Parallel 有什么区别？

强回答：

ZeRO and FSDP mainly shard training states such as parameters, gradients, and optimizer states to reduce memory usage in data-parallel training. Tensor parallel splits matrix operations across GPUs within a layer, which is important for very large models and inference too. Pipeline parallel splits layers across devices and uses micro-batches. They solve different memory and compute distribution problems.

L. Behavioral / communication#

Q25: 如果 research team 想上一个新模型，但 infra 认为风险高，你怎么推进？

强回答：

I would turn the debate into a rollout contract. We define model card, expected quality gain, latency/cost budget, safety requirements, traffic slice, canary metrics, rollback trigger, and owner. If the model is promising but expensive, we can start with offline eval, shadow traffic, low-risk tenants, or async workloads before moving to interactive production traffic.

Q26: 你如何展示 passion for efficiency and open source？

强回答：

I like reducing waste at the system boundary: fewer unnecessary media transfers, better batching, token-aware routing, model warm pools, index freshness controls, and clearer observability. For open source, I prefer small upstreamable improvements: reproducible benchmark, bug report, minimal patch, documentation, or adapter that avoids long-lived internal forks.

23. Source-backed knowledge map：面试时可以引用哪些官方概念#

这一节把官方资料转成面试可说的语言。不要在面试里背链接，但要能说出“这个判断来自哪里、为什么可信、生产上怎么落地”。

核心 sources：

  • vLLM docs：vLLM Documentation 强调 PagedAttention、continuous batching、chunked prefill、prefix caching、CUDA/HIP graphs、quantization。面试里可引用为：vLLM 是 token-serving engine，不是普通 Python wrapper。
  • Ray Serve docs：Ray Serve 定位为 scalable model serving library，支持 framework-agnostic serving、response streaming、dynamic request batching、multi-node/multi-GPU serving。面试里可引用为：Ray Serve 做 serving composition 和 Python-native distributed serving，不替代 vLLM 的 KV/token scheduler。
  • KubeRay docs：Ray on Kubernetes 推荐用 KubeRay operator 管 Ray cluster，Ray cluster 包括 head pod 和 worker pods，并支持 autoscaling。面试里可引用为：Kubernetes 管 Ray cluster lifecycle，Ray 管 distributed execution。
  • Kubernetes device plugins：Device Plugins 说明 device plugin 用于把 GPU/RDMA 等特殊硬件暴露给 kubelet。面试里可引用为：K8s 原生资源模型需要 device plugin 才能调度 GPU。
  • Kubernetes taints/tolerations：Taints and Tolerations 说明 taint 用于 repel pods，toleration 允许但不保证调度。面试里可引用为：GPU pool 隔离通常要结合 taints/tolerations、node affinity、quota。
  • NVIDIA NCCL：NCCL Overview 定位为 topology-aware inter-GPU communication primitives，包括 collectives 和 point-to-point。面试里可引用为：multi-GPU inference/training 的网络瓶颈常落在 collective communication。
  • TensorRT-LLM docs：TensorRT-LLM 覆盖 in-flight batching、chunked context/prefill、KV cache、paged attention、parallelism。面试里可引用为：TensorRT-LLM 是成熟高流量端点的性能投资。
  • SGLang docs：SGLang Documentation 强调 RadixAttention、prefix caching、multi-GPU parallelism、structured outputs。面试里可引用为：SGLang 适合 structured generation / agent / RAG program。
  • DeepSpeed ZeRO docs：DeepSpeed ZeRO 说明 ZeRO 通过 partition optimizer states、gradients、parameters 降低数据并行冗余。面试里可引用为：ZeRO/FSDP 是训练显存管理，不是 LLM serving 的完整答案。
  • PyTorch FSDP docs：PyTorch FSDP 定位为 across data parallel workers sharding module parameters。面试里可引用为：训练 checkpoint 到 serving artifact 需要 lifecycle bridge。
  • AWS SageMaker：SageMaker deployment options 和 SageMaker training 可用于 managed training/inference 对比。
  • Azure ML：Azure ML endpoints 说明 online/batch endpoints、traffic split、mirror traffic、auth/network features。
  • Google Vertex AI / Agent Platform：Gemini Enterprise Agent Platform docs 可用于解释 Google Cloud 上 agent/model platform 的 managed path。
  • NVIDIA GPU Operator：GPU Operator 说明 GPU Operator 自动管理 driver、device plugin、container toolkit、GFD、DCGM monitoring 等组件。面试里可引用为：GPU on Kubernetes 不是只装一个 pod，而是一整套 node enablement and observability。

面试里的使用方式：

I do not choose tools by popularity. I map each official capability to a system boundary: vLLM/TensorRT-LLM/SGLang for token execution, Ray/KubeRay for distributed execution, Kubernetes/device plugins/GPU Operator for hardware orchestration, NCCL for multi-GPU communication, and DeepSpeed/FSDP/PyTorch for training-time memory/parallelism.

24. Deep-dive drill：large model inference 追问怎么答#

Drill 1: 解释 prefill/decode 资源画像。

强回答：

Prefill processes all prompt tokens and can use parallel matrix operations efficiently. Decode generates one token per sequence per step, so it becomes latency-sensitive and often memory-bandwidth/KV-cache limited. This is why long prompts hurt TTFT and long generations hurt TPOT. A good serving platform separates these metrics instead of hiding them in end-to-end latency.

可以追加：

  • prefill-heavy workload 适合 chunked prefill、prefix cache、prompt compression、RAG context budget。
  • decode-heavy workload 适合 better batching、speculative decoding、smaller model fallback、output length control。
  • 对 interactive UX，TTFT 往往比 total latency 更敏感。

Drill 2: 你如何设计 admission control？

强回答：

Admission control should estimate prompt tokens, max output tokens, KV cache blocks, tenant quota, model availability, and priority. If the request would violate KV budget or SLO, the router can queue, downgrade model, reduce max tokens, switch to async mode, or return a controlled overload response.

关键 formula：

text
estimated_kv_bytes =
  num_layers * 2(K,V) * num_tokens * hidden_size * dtype_bytes

面试注意：

真实公式会因 grouped-query attention、tensor parallel、KV dtype、engine layout 而变化。 · 面试重点不是背精确 bytes，而是知道 KV cache 会随 sequence length 和并发放大。

Drill 3: 你如何解释 vLLM vs TensorRT-LLM vs SGLang？

强回答：

vLLM is the practical default for general LLM serving because it combines throughput and iteration speed. TensorRT-LLM is the performance path when the workload is stable and NVIDIA optimization cost is justified. SGLang is the structured-generation path when the application has repeated prefixes, tool/RAG programs, constrained output, and agent-style control flow.

follow-up trap：

  • 不要说 TensorRT-LLM 一定比 vLLM 好。要说 “after benchmark on target model, hardware, context distribution, and SLO”。
  • 不要说 SGLang 只是另一个 serving server。要强调 structured generation program and prefix reuse。

25. Deep-dive drill：distributed systems / parallelism 追问怎么答#

Q: 大模型训练和推理里有哪些 parallelism？

强回答：

Data parallelism replicates the model and splits data. FSDP/ZeRO shard parameters, gradients, and optimizer states to reduce memory. Tensor parallelism splits operations inside layers, which is important when one layer cannot fit or one GPU is too slow. Pipeline parallelism splits layers across devices and uses micro-batches. Expert parallelism is common in MoE. For inference, tensor/expert parallelism and KV-cache placement become especially important.

要能区分：

DDP：每个 rank 一份模型，gradient all-reduce。 · FSDP/ZeRO：参数/梯度/optimizer state 分片，forward/backward 时 gather。 · TP：矩阵乘按列/行切，依赖 all-reduce/all-gather。 · PP：按层切分，关注 pipeline bubble。 · EP：MoE expert 分布，关注 all-to-all。 · CP/SP：长上下文或 sequence 维度切分。

Q: NCCL 在这里做什么？

强回答：

NCCL provides topology-aware communication primitives for multi-GPU and multi-node workloads. In training, it is used for gradient all-reduce, reduce-scatter, all-gather, and all-to-all style communication. In inference, tensor parallel and expert parallel also rely on GPU communication. If NCCL/topology is bad, adding GPUs can reduce efficiency rather than improve latency.

debug 方向：

单机内看 NVLink/NVSwitch/PCIe topology。 · 跨机看 InfiniBand/RDMA/EFA。 · 看 collective time、straggler rank、network errors、GPU direct RDMA 配置。

Q: Ray task 设计多大合适？

强回答：

A Ray task should be large enough to amortize scheduling and serialization overhead, but small enough to retry and load-balance. For media AI, I avoid passing raw video/audio tensors through Ray object store if artifact references are enough. For GPU inference, I prefer batch-level tasks or long-lived actors to keep model warm.

26. Deep-dive drill：Kubernetes / GPU orchestration 追问怎么答#

Q: Kubernetes 怎么知道一台机器有 GPU？

强回答：

Kubernetes uses device plugins to advertise special hardware resources such as GPUs to kubelet. For NVIDIA GPUs, the NVIDIA device plugin exposes nvidia.com/gpu resources, while GPU Operator can manage the broader stack such as drivers, container toolkit, device plugin, GPU feature discovery, and DCGM monitoring.

注意：

resources.limits.nvidia.com/gpu: 1 只是请求 GPU 的起点。 · 还需要 node labels、taints/tolerations、affinity、runtime、driver compatibility、monitoring。

Q: GPU node pool 怎么设计？

强回答：

I separate node pools by GPU type and workload class. For example, L4/A10 for embedding and smaller models, A100/H100 for large LLMs, protected pools for interactive traffic, preemptible pools for batch. Taints/tolerations repel generic workloads, node affinity attracts the correct workloads, and quotas prevent one tenant or team from consuming all GPUs.

Q: Pod running 为什么不等于 model ready？

强回答：

A pod can be running while the model is still downloading weights, loading into GPU memory, compiling kernels, building TensorRT engine, warming KV cache, or waiting for tensor-parallel peers. Readiness should check model-loaded and warm status, not just process health.

Q: 你怎么设计 ModelDeployment CRD？

字段建议：

yaml
apiVersion: inference.platform/v1
kind: ModelDeployment
spec:
  modelRef: llama-prod
  engine: vllm
  replicas:
    min: 2
    max: 20
  resources:
    gpuType: h100
    gpuCount: 1
    memoryGiB: 80
  routing:
    latencyClass: interactive
    tenantPolicy: enterprise
  autoscaling:
    metrics:
      - pending_tokens
      - ttft_p95
      - kv_cache_usage
status:
  phase: Ready
  loadedReplicas: 4
  warmingReplicas: 1

controller 原则：

reconcile idempotent。 · status update rate-limited。 · finalizer 清理 routing entry 和 model cache。 · rollout 需要 canary/rollback。

27. Deep-dive drill：cloud ML infra 追问怎么答#

Q: SageMaker/Azure ML/Vertex AI 和自建平台怎么选？

强回答：

Managed ML platforms are good when the problem fits standard training jobs, managed endpoints, experiment tracking, security defaults, and team productivity. Self-managed Kubernetes/Ray/vLLM/Triton is better when I need custom token-aware routing, multi-engine serving, GPU-aware scheduling, strict product integration, or cross-cloud portability. I usually keep contracts portable even when using managed services.

要能说出 provider 术语：

  • AWS：SageMaker Training、real-time endpoints、multi-model endpoints、S3、IAM、KMS、EKS、MSK。
  • Azure：Azure ML jobs、online endpoints、batch endpoints、traffic split、mirror traffic、Key Vault、AKS。
  • GCP：Vertex AI / Agent Platform、custom training、endpoints、model registry、GCS、GKE、Pub/Sub。

Q: managed endpoint 最大的限制是什么？

强回答：

Managed endpoints simplify deployment, scaling, and security, but may not expose enough control for token-aware scheduling, KV-cache-aware routing, custom engine metrics, cross-engine fallback, or advanced GPU packing. For high-scale LLM platforms, the control plane often needs to sit above or beside managed endpoints.

28. Deep-dive drill：monitoring / operations 追问怎么答#

Q: 你会给 LLM inference 配哪些 alerts？

建议 alerts：

  • TTFT p95/p99 超阈值。
  • TPOT p95/p99 超阈值。
  • pending tokens 持续增长。
  • KV cache usage 高且 free blocks 低。
  • OOM / CUDA error / NCCL error。
  • model loading failure / warmup timeout。
  • fallback rate 异常升高。
  • cost per successful output 异常升高。
  • safety block / policy deny 异常变化。
  • retrieval zero-result / index freshness lag。

强回答：

I alert on user-visible SLO, engine saturation, data freshness, quality, and cost. For AI systems, a service can be technically up but product-broken because retrieval is stale, model quality regressed, or token cost exploded.

Q: 如何做 load test？

强回答：

I would replay realistic token distributions, not synthetic QPS only. The load test should include short prompts, long prompts, long outputs, streaming, cancellation, RAG/tool latency, hot tenant spikes, and mixed models. The output should report TTFT, TPOT, throughput, queue wait, KV pressure, GPU utilization, error rate, and cost.

load test cases：

90% short chat + 10% long context。 · burst from one tenant。 · many short requests plus one very long request。 · model rollout with cold replicas。 · retrieval dependency slow。 · tool timeout。

29. Deep-dive drill：language and coding 追问怎么答#

Q: 让你现场写一个 router，你会怎么写？

回答结构：

I would define typed request/route models, separate policy from execution, and make routing decisions explainable in logs/traces.

简化代码：

python
def route(req, policy, metrics):
    candidates = policy.allowed_models(req.tenant_id, req.task_type, req.modality)
    scored = []
    for c in candidates:
        m = metrics.get(c.endpoint)
        score = (
            0.30 * slo_score(m.ttft_p95, req.latency_class)
            + 0.25 * capacity_score(m.pending_tokens, m.kv_cache_usage)
            + 0.20 * capability_score(c, req)
            + 0.15 * locality_score(c, req.region, req.prefix_hash)
            + 0.10 * cost_score(c)
        )
        scored.append((score, c))
    return max(scored, key=lambda x: x[0])[1]

追问点：

policy.allowed_models 支持 tenant/model allowlist。 · route decision 要写入 OpenTelemetry span。 · fallback path 要可控，不能 silent downgrade 造成质量风险。

Q: 让你写 Kubernetes controller，你会强调什么？

强回答：

I would use Go with controller-runtime. The reconcile loop reads desired state, compares actual state, creates or patches child resources, updates status, and requeues only when needed. The key is idempotency, rate limiting, ownership references, finalizers, and not doing long blocking work inside reconcile.

Go concepts：

context.Context 处理 timeout/cancel。 · client.Get/Create/Patch/Status().Update。 · controllerutil.SetControllerReference。 · workqueue rate limiter。 · structured logging and metrics。

Q: Python ML infra 代码最容易踩什么坑？

回答：

blocking I/O 放在 async path。 · 大对象 serialization 进入 Ray object store。 · 每个 request 重建 tokenizer/model client。 · 没有 timeout/retry/circuit breaker。 · 把 prompt/raw PII 直接打 log。 · 不区分 model error、policy deny、capacity reject。

30. System design prompts：面试官可能直接让你设计什么#

这些题要练到可以白板讲 10-20 分钟。每道题都按 requirements -> architecture -> data/control plane -> scheduling -> failure handling -> metrics -> tradeoffs 回答。

Prompt 1: Design a Kubernetes-native LLM inference platform.

回答骨架：

  • Requirements：multi-tenant、multi-model、interactive + batch、GPU cost control、safe rollout、observability。
  • API：ModelDeployment、ModelEndpoint、RoutePolicy、AutoscalingPolicy。
  • Control plane：model registry、engine adapter、router config、quota、rollout controller。
  • Data plane：vLLM/SGLang/TensorRT-LLM replicas、Triton encoder/reranker replicas、Ray workers。
  • Scheduling：GPU type、KV cache budget、pending tokens、tenant priority、region、prefix cache locality。
  • Autoscaling：pod autoscaling 看 queue/token/KV 指标，node autoscaling 看 pending GPU pods。
  • Monitoring：TTFT、TPOT、tokens/sec、KV usage、GPU HBM、fallback rate、cost。
  • Failure：model load failure、OOM、NCCL error、router overload、bad rollout。

强结尾：

The core is an engine-agnostic but token-aware control plane. Kubernetes manages pods and nodes; the platform manages model semantics, token queues, routing, and safe rollout.

Prompt 2: Design a GPU-aware scheduler for mixed inference workloads.

回答骨架：

Workloads：chat LLM、embedding、reranking、batch enrichment、agent workflow。 · Queueing：priority queue by latency class，weighted fair queue by tenant。 · Placement：GPU type、memory、topology、model warm state、node locality。 · Admission：estimate tokens and KV cache before accepting。 · Preemption：batch 可抢占，interactive 尽量不抢占。 · Isolation：tenant quota、namespace、node pool、model allowlist。 · Metrics：pending tokens、queue wait、GPU memory、KV free blocks、tenant fairness。

Prompt 3: Design Ray on Kubernetes for large-scale batch embedding.

回答骨架：

Input：asset change events + backfill manifest。 · Orchestration：KubeRay 管 Ray cluster，Ray Data 做 map_batches，Triton 做 embedding。 · Data movement：object store 只传 metadata/reference，大 media 在 artifact store。 · Failure：task retry、bad asset quarantine、idempotent vector upsert。 · Scaling：KEDA/Karpenter 根据 queue/backlog 和 pending pods 扩容。 · Output：embedding version、index version、quality metrics、publish event。

Prompt 4: Design observability for LLM/RAG/agent platform.

回答骨架：

  • Trace：request -> planner -> retrieval -> rerank -> LLM -> tool -> artifact。
  • Metrics：engine metrics、GPU metrics、workflow metrics、retrieval metrics、quality metrics、cost metrics。
  • Logs：structured logs with request_id/workflow_id，redacted prompt。
  • Events：Kafka audit/eval/feedback events。
  • Dashboards：SLO、capacity、quality、cost、safety。
  • Alerts：TTFT/TPOT、pending tokens、index freshness、policy failure、cost spike。

Prompt 5: Design a cross-cloud ML platform using AWS/Azure/GCP.

回答骨架：

Portable contracts：model artifact、endpoint contract、event schema、observability schema。 · Cloud-specific adapters：S3/Blob/GCS、IAM/Entra/GCP IAM、SageMaker/Azure ML/Vertex。 · Deployment：managed endpoints for standard workloads，自建 K8s for custom LLM engines。 · Governance：region/data residency、KMS、audit、network isolation。 · Tradeoff：managed productivity vs custom control。

31. Scheduling algorithms and routing policies：别只说 autoscaling#

面试官问 scheduling 时，可以从 algorithm 和 policy 两层讲。

Core algorithms / policies：

  • Bin packing：尽量把 workload 放满节点，提高 utilization。风险是 noisy neighbor 和碎片。
  • Spread / anti-affinity：把 replicas 分散到不同 node/zone，提高可用性。风险是成本和 locality 变差。
  • Priority queue：interactive 高优先级，batch 低优先级。风险是低优先级长期 starvation。
  • Weighted fair queuing：tenant/team 按权重共享资源，避免大客户或单团队挤占。
  • Least outstanding tokens：LLM routing 比 least requests 更合理，因为请求 token shape 差异大。
  • Cache-aware routing：优先路由到 prefix cache/model warm/cache locality 命中的 replica。
  • Power-of-two choices：随机挑两个候选 replica，选更空的，简单且降低热点。
  • Admission control：在进入系统前根据 quota/KV/SLO 拒绝、降级或转 async。
  • Backpressure：downstream 慢时 upstream 减速，避免 queue 无限膨胀。
  • Load shedding：系统过载时保护核心流量，丢弃或降级低优先级请求。

LLM router scoring 可以这样解释：

text
score(replica) =
  w1 * kv_free_ratio
  + w2 * inverse_queue_wait
  + w3 * prefix_cache_hit
  + w4 * region_affinity
  + w5 * tenant_priority
  - w6 * estimated_cost

追问：为什么不能只用 round-robin？

Round-robin assumes requests are similar. LLM requests are not similar: prompt length, output length, KV cache usage, tool calls, and tenant priority can differ by orders of magnitude. A good router should be token-aware and cache-aware.

追问：为什么不能只用 HPA？

HPA usually reacts to aggregate metrics such as CPU or custom metrics. It does not by itself understand model warmup, KV cache pressure, token backlog, or GPU topology. We can use HPA/KEDA/Karpenter as mechanisms, but the AI platform must provide the right metrics and policies.

32. Failure drills：按症状快速定位问题#

Symptom: TTFT p99 suddenly high.

Likely causes：

prefill queue 堵塞。 · prompt length distribution 变长。 · prefix cache miss rate 上升。 · cold model replicas 增多。 · tokenizer / gateway / retrieval 变慢。

Actions：

split trace into queue/prefill/retrieval。 · check prompt token histogram。 · check model rollout and cache hit。 · route long-context traffic to async or separate pool。

Symptom: TPOT high but TTFT normal.

Likely causes：

decode memory bandwidth bottleneck。 · batch size / max_num_seqs 不合适。 · GPU HBM pressure。 · tensor parallel communication overhead。 · output length spike。

Actions：

check decode tokens/sec。 · inspect GPU HBM/DCGM metrics。 · check NCCL time and topology。 · cap max output tokens or use smaller model。

Symptom: GPU utilization high but useful tokens/sec low.

Likely causes：

memory-bound decode。 · inefficient batching。 · excessive communication。 · too many small kernels。 · CPU/tokenizer/logging bottleneck hiding behind busy GPU。

Actions：

compare prefill/decode throughput。 · profile kernel/network/CPU。 · check batch token count and sequence count。 · measure cost per accepted output。

Symptom: Ray object store OOM.

Likely causes：

raw media/tensors pushed through object store。 · task outputs held by references。 · spilling too slow。 · task fan-out too aggressive。

Actions：

pass artifact references instead of blobs。 · reduce task granularity/fan-out。 · inspect object refs and spilling。 · move large artifacts to S3/GCS/Blob。

Symptom: Kubernetes GPU pods Pending.

Likely causes：

no matching GPU node type。 · taints/tolerations mismatch。 · node affinity too strict。 · quota exhausted。 · Karpenter/cluster autoscaler cannot provision instance type。 · device plugin not reporting GPU。

Actions：

kubectl describe pod 看 scheduling events。 · check node labels/taints。 · check resource quota。 · check device plugin/GPU Operator。 · check cloud capacity and instance type constraints。

Symptom: NCCL timeout.

Likely causes：

rank failure。 · network partition。 · topology mismatch。 · driver/CUDA/NCCL version mismatch。 · one worker slower causing collective hang。

Actions：

identify failing rank。 · check node/GPU health。 · check NCCL logs and interface selection。 · verify topology and security groups/firewall。 · run small collective test。

Symptom: RAG quality drops but serving is healthy.

Likely causes：

stale index。 · embedding version mismatch。 · bad chunking rollout。 · ACL filter too strict。 · reranker regression。 · query distribution shift。

Actions：

compare index_version/embedding_version。 · run golden query set。 · inspect zero-result and citation quality。 · rollback index or reranker。

33. Observability and metrics implementation：Triton / Ray / DCGM / OTel 怎么接#

官方能力映射：

  • Triton：model repository、per-model scheduler、dynamic batching、model config、HTTP/gRPC endpoint、Prometheus metrics。
  • Ray Serve：Ray dashboard、serve status、built-in Serve metrics、logs/Loki integration。
  • DCGM Exporter：Go-based exporter，通过 /metrics 暴露 GPU telemetry 给 Prometheus。
  • OpenTelemetry：vendor-neutral traces、metrics、logs，用 shared context 串起 request path。

Implementation pattern：

text
Application instrumentation
  -> OpenTelemetry SDK
  -> OTel Collector
  -> tracing backend

vLLM / Triton / Ray / app metrics
  -> Prometheus scrape
  -> Grafana dashboards

GPU node telemetry
  -> DCGM Exporter
  -> Prometheus

Workflow and quality events
  -> Kafka
  -> warehouse / eval pipeline

Metrics you should name：

  • vLLM/SGLang：TTFT、TPOT、prompt tokens/sec、output tokens/sec、pending requests/tokens、KV cache usage、prefix cache hit。
  • Triton：request count、queue duration、compute input/infer/output duration、dynamic batch size、model load status。
  • Ray：task backlog、actor health、object store memory、spilling、placement group pending、Serve replica latency。
  • DCGM：GPU utilization、memory used/free、SM occupancy/profiling metrics、PCIe/NVLink throughput、temperature、power、ECC error。
  • Kubernetes：pod pending, restart count, node pressure, quota, HPA/KEDA scaling event, Karpenter node provisioning。
  • Product quality：accept rate、thumbs up/down、eval score、grounding/citation score、fallback rate。

Alert principle：

Alert on symptoms users feel, saturation signals operators can act on, and quality/cost regressions product teams care about. Do not alert only on raw GPU utilization.

34. Kubernetes autoscaling and cluster management：HPA / KEDA / Karpenter / scheduler framework#

不要把 autoscaling 讲成一个东西。它有不同层：

  • HPA：scale pods based on resource/custom metrics。
  • KEDA：event-driven autoscaling，可以按 queue/event backlog 驱动 Kubernetes workloads scale in/out，适合 Kafka/SQS/EventHub/backlog 类任务。
  • Karpenter：node provisioning，根据 unschedulable pods 和 constraints 快速创建合适节点，适合云上 GPU node pool 弹性。
  • Cluster Autoscaler：传统 node autoscaler，根据 pending pods 扩节点。
  • Kubernetes scheduler framework：可插拔 scheduling plugin architecture，用于更深度的调度扩展。
  • Custom platform router：应用层 request routing，处理 token/KV/SLO/model semantics。

如何组合：

text
Application router
  -> routes request by token/KV/SLO

HPA/KEDA
  -> changes replica count by metrics/backlog

Kubernetes scheduler
  -> places pods onto nodes

Karpenter/Cluster Autoscaler
  -> provisions nodes when pods cannot fit

GPU Operator/device plugin
  -> makes GPUs visible and observable

面试回答：

I use autoscaling as a multi-layer control loop. The app router protects request SLO, HPA/KEDA changes replicas, Karpenter provisions GPU nodes, and Kubernetes schedules pods. For LLM workloads, the critical part is feeding these loops token-aware and GPU-aware metrics, otherwise the system scales too late or scales the wrong resource.

Common pitfalls：

HPA 看 CPU，但 GPU serving CPU 很低。 · Karpenter 能开节点，但模型加载仍然慢。 · KEDA scale-to-zero 对 cold-start-sensitive inference 不一定合适。 · scheduler 能放 pod，但不知道 model warm/cold。 · node scale-up 成功不代表 endpoint ready。

35. High-performance networking and parallelism drill#

必须会讲的 collectives：

all-reduce：所有 rank 汇总并得到结果，DDP gradient sync 常见。 · reduce-scatter：reduce 后分片结果，ZeRO/FSDP 常见。 · all-gather：收集所有分片，FSDP parameter materialization 常见。 · all-to-all：MoE expert routing 常见。 · broadcast：从一个 rank 分发到其他 rank。 · send/recv：点对点通信，pipeline parallel 或自定义通信中常见。

网络和拓扑：

NVLink/NVSwitch：单机多 GPU 高带宽。 · PCIe：GPU/CPU/设备互连，带宽低于 NVLink。 · InfiniBand/RDMA/AWS EFA：跨机低延迟高带宽。 · Topology-aware placement：TP/PP/EP replica 要尽量放到合适 GPU group。

面试回答：

Parallelism is only useful if communication overhead is lower than the compute saved. For tensor parallel inference, each token step may involve communication, so topology matters. For pipeline parallel, bubble and micro-batch matter. For MoE, all-to-all can dominate. I would benchmark on the actual cluster topology before assuming more GPUs means lower latency.

Debug checklist：

Compare single-GPU vs multi-GPU latency。 · Inspect NCCL logs and selected interfaces。 · Check rank placement on nodes。 · Check NVLink/NVSwitch availability。 · Check cross-zone traffic accidentally happening。 · Run synthetic collective benchmark before blaming model code。

Kubernetes：

bash

kubectl describe pod <pod>
kubectl describe node <node>
kubectl top pod -n inference
kubectl get events -A --sort-by=.lastTimestamp

GPU / NVIDIA：

vLLM / LLM serving：

bash

curl http://router/metrics

What to say：

I do not debug from dashboards alone. I usually start from the request trace, then check engine metrics, Kubernetes events, GPU telemetry, and recent rollout/config changes.

37. 30/60/90 day plan for this role#

这个可以用于 recruiter / hiring manager / tech lead 面试结尾。

First 30 days：

  • Understand current model serving architecture, traffic shape, SLOs, GPU fleet, cloud account boundaries, and incident history。
  • Read dashboards, oncall runbooks, deployment pipelines, and cost reports。
  • Shadow one model rollout and one production incident。
  • Build a map of control plane, data plane, routing, monitoring, and ownership boundaries。

Days 31-60：

  • Improve one high-signal dashboard: token metrics + GPU + routing + quality/cost。
  • Add or refine one load test with realistic token distributions。
  • Fix one operational pain point: model warmup, queue visibility, fallback clarity, or index freshness。
  • Write a design doc for token-aware routing or GPU-aware autoscaling improvement。

Days 61-90：

  • Ship a measurable platform improvement: lower TTFT, higher GPU utilization, safer rollout, lower cost per token, or faster model onboarding。
  • Create a reusable runbook for inference incidents。
  • Propose a roadmap for engine abstraction, multi-cloud resource management, or open-source contribution。

Strong close：

My first goal would be to make the system more observable before making it more complex. Once we can see token demand, GPU pressure, routing decisions, and quality/cost outcomes clearly, optimization becomes much safer.

38. Capacity planning and GPU memory math：面试里最能体现 hands-on#

这类题很常见：How many GPUs do we need? 不要直接猜数字，要先列变量。

LLM serving capacity 粗算：

text
required_replicas =
  peak_output_tokens_per_sec
  / measured_output_tokens_per_sec_per_replica
  * headroom
  * redundancy

但是真实系统还要看：

prompt token distribution，不只是 output tokens。 · TTFT SLO 和 TPOT SLO。 · KV cache memory。 · model warmup time。 · replica failure / zone failure。 · traffic burst 和 hot tenant。 · batch jobs 是否可以用 idle capacity。

KV cache 粗算：

text
kv_cache_bytes_per_token =
  num_layers * 2(K,V) * hidden_size * dtype_bytes

total_kv_cache =
  kv_cache_bytes_per_token * concurrent_tokens

实际要修正：

MHA / MQA / GQA 的 KV heads 不同。 · tensor parallel 会改变每卡 KV 分布。 · engine 可能使用 paged KV cache、block allocator、FP8 KV cache。 · max context length 越大，不代表每个 request 都占满，但 admission 必须有预算。

模型权重粗算：

text
weight_memory =
  num_parameters * bytes_per_parameter

例子：

7B FP16 大约 14GB weights。 · 70B FP16 大约 140GB weights，需要 tensor parallel 或量化。 · INT8/FP8/INT4 降低权重 memory，但不一定同比降低 KV cache、activation、通信和质量风险。

面试回答模板：

I would first measure tokens/sec per replica on the target model, hardware, context distribution, and engine. Then I would add headroom for bursts, failures, model warmup, and long-context KV cache. I would not size purely by QPS because two requests can differ by 100x in token cost.

39. Benchmarking plan：如何证明一个 engine 或 scheduler 更好#

面试官可能问：How do you compare vLLM vs SGLang vs TensorRT-LLM? 强回答不是“看 benchmark”，而是设计 benchmark。

Benchmark dimensions：

model：same model weights、same tokenizer、same precision。 · hardware：same GPU type、same topology、same driver/CUDA。 · workload：short prompt、long prompt、long output、RAG prompt、structured output、streaming。 · concurrency：low/medium/high，burst traffic，hot tenant。 · metrics：TTFT、TPOT、tokens/sec/GPU、p95/p99 latency、OOM、cost/token、quality drift。 · stability：soak test、model reload、node failure、rolling upgrade。

Benchmark cases：

text
Case A: 90% short chat, 10% long context
Case B: many shared-prefix RAG requests
Case C: structured JSON/tool-call generation
Case D: long output generation
Case E: mixed interactive + batch
Case F: cold start and rolling deploy

结果解读：

vLLM 在通用 serving 上可能最省心。 · SGLang 在 shared prefix/structured generation 上可能更强。 · TensorRT-LLM 在稳定高流量 endpoint 上可能 cost/token 更低。 · 如果 quality 变差或 rollout 复杂度过高，低 latency 不一定值得。

一句话：

I benchmark engines against the product workload, not against a synthetic average request. The best engine for short chat may not be best for long-context RAG, structured agent calls, or batch enrichment.

40. Resource quota, isolation, and security：enterprise infra 必问#

Kubernetes 层：

ResourceQuota：限制 namespace 总资源，例如 CPU、memory、GPU、pods、services。 · LimitRange：给容器默认/最大 request 和 limit。 · NetworkPolicy：限制 pod ingress/egress，默认 deny 再 allow 必要路径。 · RBAC：限制谁能创建/更新 ModelDeployment、Secret、ServiceAccount。 · Pod Security：限制 privileged、hostPath、root user。 · Node pool isolation：敏感 tenant 或高优先级 workload 独立 node pool。

AI platform 层：

tenant quota：tokens/min、requests/min、GPU-hours/day、batch backlog。 · model allowlist：哪些 tenant 可以用哪些模型。 · data policy：region、PII、copyright、retention。 · tool permission：agent tool call 需要 scoped credential。 · audit event：每次 retrieval、generation、tool action 都能追踪。

面试回答：

I treat isolation as layered. Kubernetes gives namespace, network, RBAC, quota, and node isolation. The AI platform adds model access, token quota, data policy, tool permission, and audit. For enterprise AI, both layers are necessary.

41. Ray placement groups and actor design：证明你真的懂 Ray#

Ray 的 placement group 用于把一组 task/actor 资源作为 bundles 调度，适合：

multi-GPU model parallel actors。 · pipeline stages 需要 co-location 或 spread。 · 保证一组资源同时可用。

策略：

PACK：尽量放一起，减少通信，适合 tightly-coupled stages。 · SPREAD：分散放，提升 fault tolerance。 · STRICT_PACK：必须同一节点，适合单机多 GPU。 · STRICT_SPREAD：必须分散，适合 replicas 高可用。

示意代码：

python
import ray
from ray.util.placement_group import placement_group

pg = placement_group(
    bundles=[{"CPU": 4, "GPU": 1}, {"CPU": 4, "GPU": 1}],
    strategy="STRICT_PACK",
)
ray.get(pg.ready())

@ray.remote(num_cpus=4, num_gpus=1)
class ModelWorker:
    def generate(self, batch):
        return run_model(batch)

workers = [
    ModelWorker.options(
        scheduling_strategy=ray.util.scheduling_strategies.PlacementGroupSchedulingStrategy(
            placement_group=pg,
            placement_group_bundle_index=i,
        )
    ).remote()
    for i in range(2)
]

面试重点：

Ray placement groups are useful when task-level scheduling is not enough and I need gang-like resource placement. But I still need Kubernetes-level node provisioning and GPU topology awareness underneath.

42. Prometheus instrumentation：你会暴露哪些 metrics#

官方 Prometheus instrumentation 建议是：每个 library/subsystem/service 至少暴露足够 metrics，方便从 alert 回到代码。

AI router metrics：

text
ai_router_requests_total{tenant,model,route,status}
ai_router_request_latency_seconds_bucket{route}
ai_router_pending_tokens{model,priority}
ai_router_fallback_total{from_model,to_model,reason}
ai_router_rejected_total{reason}

Workflow metrics：

text
ai_workflow_duration_seconds_bucket{workflow_type,status}
ai_workflow_step_retries_total{step_type}
ai_workflow_stuck_total{step_type}
ai_artifact_write_failures_total{artifact_type}

Retrieval metrics：

text
rag_retrieval_latency_seconds_bucket{index_version}
rag_zero_result_total{tenant,index_version}
rag_rerank_latency_seconds_bucket{model_version}
rag_index_freshness_lag_seconds{tenant}

LLM serving metrics：

text
llm_ttft_seconds_bucket{model,engine}
llm_tpot_seconds_bucket{model,engine}
llm_input_tokens_total{model}
llm_output_tokens_total{model}
llm_kv_cache_usage_ratio{model,replica}

Metric design pitfalls：

label cardinality 太高，例如 raw user_id、prompt_hash、asset_id。 · 只看 average，不看 histogram/p95/p99。 · 没有把 route/model/version 作为安全 label。 · cost 和 quality metrics 缺失。

43. CUDA basics for non-kernel interview：懂边界就够加分#

如果你不是 CUDA kernel engineer，可以诚实但要讲得有深度：

I am not claiming to hand-write every attention kernel, but I understand the GPU performance model enough to work with engine/kernel teams and debug system bottlenecks.

必须会解释：

kernel launch overhead：太多小 kernel 会浪费调度开销。 · memory coalescing：相邻线程访问连续内存更高效。 · shared memory：低延迟片上 memory，可减少 global memory 访问。 · warp divergence：同一 warp 内分支不同导致执行效率下降。 · tensor cores：加速矩阵乘，依赖数据类型和 layout。 · occupancy：SM 上活跃 warps/blocks 的程度，不是越高越一定好。 · HBM bandwidth：decode 和 KV cache 相关 workload 常受 memory bandwidth 限制。 · NCCL collective：多 GPU 通信开销可能主导 tensor parallel。

LLM inference 关联：

attention kernel 受 sequence length、KV layout、batching 影响。 · quantized matmul 降低 memory bandwidth，但可能影响 quality 和 kernel support。 · paged KV cache 降低 fragmentation，但 block size 和 allocator 也会影响性能。 · speculative decoding 不是免费，draft model accept rate 很关键。

44. Mock interview rapid-fire：短问短答#

Q: HPA, KEDA, Karpenter 各自做什么？

A: HPA 扩 pod，KEDA 按事件/backlog 驱动 workload 扩缩，Karpenter/Cluster Autoscaler 扩 node。LLM 平台还需要 token-aware router，因为这些工具不理解 KV cache 和 prompt length。

Q: Device plugin 和 GPU Operator 区别？

A: device plugin 把 GPU 暴露给 kubelet 作为 schedulable resource。GPU Operator 自动管理更完整的 NVIDIA stack，例如 driver、container toolkit、device plugin、GPU feature discovery、DCGM monitoring。

Q: Ray actor 和 task 怎么选？

A: stateless、短 compute 用 task；有状态、需要 warm model/client/GPU resource 的用 actor。模型推理 worker 通常用 actor。

Q: Ray object store 适合放什么？

A: 适合共享中等大小 immutable objects 和 batch 数据，不适合无限制塞 raw video/audio/media blobs。大文件应该在 artifact store，Ray 传 references。

Q: NetworkPolicy 能解决所有多租户隔离吗？

A: 不能。它限制 pod 网络流量，但还需要 RBAC、namespace、quota、secret isolation、IAM、data policy、application-level tenant checks。

Q: Prometheus label 最大坑是什么？

A: high cardinality。不要把 raw user_id、request_id、asset_id 作为 label；这些放 trace/log，metrics label 用 bounded dimensions。

Q: 为什么 model readiness 不能只看 HTTP 200？

A: 进程活着不代表模型 loaded/warmed，不代表 tensor-parallel peers ready，不代表 engine 有 KV capacity，也不代表 router 已接入。

Q: GPU utilization 低一定不好吗？

A: 不一定。interactive workload 为低 latency 留 headroom 是合理的。关键看 SLO、cost per useful token、queue wait、batch opportunity。

Q: GPU utilization 高一定好吗？

A: 也不一定。可能是 memory-bound、通信-bound、低 useful tokens/sec，或者 batch 任务挤压 interactive traffic。

45. Incident postmortem template：展示 ownership#

面试中讲 incident 时可用这个结构：

text
Title:
Impact:
Timeline:
Detection:
Root cause:
Contributing factors:
What worked:
What failed:
Immediate mitigation:
Long-term fixes:
Metrics/alerts added:
Owner and due dates:
Customer/product communication:

AI infra postmortem 要额外包含：

affected model/version/prompt/index/engine。 · token cost impact。 · quality/safety impact。 · tenant/customer blast radius。 · rollback/canary gate 是否生效。 · 是否需要重新 eval 或重建 index。

强表达：

I try to make postmortems produce better control loops: better admission control, safer canary, clearer ownership, more actionable metrics, or simpler rollback. The goal is not just to fix one incident, but to reduce the class of failures.

46. Batch scheduling ecosystem：Kueue / Volcano / Slurm / Ray Jobs 怎么讲#

AI infra 面试里，batch scheduling 是高频追问。不要只说 Kubernetes scheduler，因为 training、batch embedding、eval、backfill 这类 workload 有 job-level semantics。

Kueue：

Kubernetes-native job queueing system，面向 batch、HPC、AI/ML。 · 管理 quota 和 job admission：什么时候 wait、什么时候 admit、什么时候 preempt。 · 适合多租户 batch jobs，共享 GPU cluster，控制 team quota。 · 它不替代 pod-level scheduler，而是在 job-level 决定是否允许创建 pods。

Volcano：

  • Kubernetes-native batch scheduling system。
  • 强项：gang scheduling、queue management、fair-share、binpack、NUMA-aware、topology-aware、device sharing、AI/Big Data workloads。
  • 适合 distributed training，需要一组 pods 同时启动，否则一个 worker 起不来整个 job 卡住。

Slurm：

  • HPC 传统强者，适合超算/HPC cluster、MPI、bare-metal GPU cluster。
  • 强项：成熟的 queue/accounting/partition/job scheduling。
  • 弱点：Kubernetes-native app、microservice、cloud elasticity、multi-tenant product integration 不如 K8s 生态自然。

Ray Jobs / RayCluster：

更偏 distributed Python/ML runtime。 · 适合 task/actor graph、batch inference、embedding、RL rollout、eval fan-out。 · 需要 KubeRay/Kueue/Volcano/K8s 来处理底层 cluster resource 和 quota。

面试回答：

I separate pod scheduling from job admission. Kubernetes schedules pods; Kueue manages batch job admission and quotas; Volcano adds gang/fair-share/topology-aware scheduling for AI jobs; Ray manages distributed Python execution once resources are available. For training jobs, gang scheduling and quota fairness matter more than simple pod placement.

选择建议：

online inference：Kubernetes scheduler + app router + HPA/KEDA/Karpenter。 · batch embedding/backfill：Kueue + Ray Jobs/Ray Data。 · distributed training：Volcano/Kueue + PyTorchJob/RayJob + NCCL/EFA。 · HPC/bare metal：Slurm 或 Slurm + Kubernetes hybrid。

47. GPU sharing：MIG / MPS / time-slicing / full GPU 怎么选#

GPU sharing 是资源利用率和 isolation 的核心话题。

Full GPU：

最简单，隔离最好，适合 large LLM、latency-sensitive inference、training。 · 缺点是小模型/低流量服务会浪费。

MIG：

Multi-Instance GPU，把支持的 NVIDIA GPU 分成多个硬件隔离 GPU instances。 · 每个 MIG instance 有独立的 compute/memory/cache slice，QoS 更强。 · 适合 small/medium models、embedding/classifier、multi-tenant isolation。 · 缺点是配置不够弹性，大模型无法跨很多小 MIG slice，重新切分可能影响 node。

MPS：

CUDA Multi-Process Service，让多个 CUDA processes 共享 GPU execution context。 · 适合多个小 CUDA workload 并发，提高 SM 利用率。 · isolation 弱于 MIG，不严格隔离 memory capacity/bandwidth。

Time-slicing：

多 pod 轮流使用 GPU。 · 适合 dev/test、低优先级 workload。 · 对 latency-sensitive serving 风险较高。

回答模板：

For production isolation, I prefer full GPU or MIG depending on model size and SLO. MPS/time-slicing can improve utilization for small or dev workloads, but they are weaker isolation mechanisms. For enterprise inference, I would not mix high-priority interactive LLM traffic with untrusted batch jobs on the same shared GPU without strong policy and metrics.

48. PyTorch distributed implementation：torch.distributed / torchrun / checkpoint#

必须能讲清楚 distributed training 的启动和通信。

核心概念：

torch.distributed：提供多进程、多节点 communication primitives。 · torchrun：PyTorch elastic launch 工具，可启动单机/多机多进程训练。 · rank：全局进程序号。 · local_rank：节点内 GPU/process 序号。 · world_size：总进程数。 · process group：通信组。 · backend：GPU 常用 NCCL，CPU 常用 Gloo。

极简 DDP skeleton：

python
import os
import torch
import torch.distributed as dist
from torch.nn.parallel import DistributedDataParallel as DDP

def main():
    dist.init_process_group(backend="nccl")
    local_rank = int(os.environ["LOCAL_RANK"])
    torch.cuda.set_device(local_rank)

    model = build_model().cuda(local_rank)
    model = DDP(model, device_ids=[local_rank])

    sampler = torch.utils.data.distributed.DistributedSampler(dataset)
    loader = torch.utils.data.DataLoader(dataset, sampler=sampler, batch_size=bs)

    for batch in loader:
        loss = model(batch).loss
        loss.backward()
        optimizer.step()
        optimizer.zero_grad()

    dist.destroy_process_group()

launch example：

bash
torchrun --nnodes=2 --nproc-per-node=8 --rdzv_backend=c10d --rdzv_endpoint=$MASTER:29500 train.py

面试重点：

每个 process 通常绑定一张 GPU。 · DDP 通过 all-reduce 同步 gradient。 · FSDP/ZeRO 通过 sharding 降低 memory。 · checkpoint 要保存 model、optimizer、scheduler、rng、epoch/step。 · elastic/fault-tolerant training 需要 rendezvous 和 restart 策略。

49. Cloud high-performance networking：EFA / RDMA / GPUDirect 怎么讲#

跨机训练/推理的瓶颈常常不是 GPU，而是 network。

RDMA：

Remote Direct Memory Access，允许绕过 CPU/kernel 的高效内存访问。 · 降低 latency 和 CPU overhead。

GPUDirect RDMA：

让第三方 PCIe 设备，如 network adapter，直接访问 GPU memory。 · 减少 GPU memory -> CPU memory -> NIC 的 copy path。 · 对 multi-node GPU workloads 很重要。

AWS EFA：

Elastic Fabric Adapter，AWS EC2 上面向 HPC/ML 的高性能网络接口。 · 支持 NCCL/MPI 等，通过 Libfabric 集成。 · 在 EKS/EC2 上做多节点训练时经常要验证 EFA + NCCL 配置。

面试回答：

For multi-node GPU workloads, I check whether the bottleneck is compute, memory bandwidth, or communication. If tensor parallel or distributed training spans nodes, NCCL and network topology become first-order concerns. On AWS I would look at EFA-enabled instances, NCCL tests, security group configuration, driver/CUDA/NCCL compatibility, and whether traffic accidentally crosses zones.

debug checklist：

single-node baseline vs multi-node。 · NCCL tests。 · nvidia-smi topo -m。 · NIC selection / NCCL env vars。 · EFA/IB device visible。 · same AZ / placement group。 · security group allows required traffic。

50. Advanced request routing：multi-model、multi-LoRA、multi-region#

生产 routing 不只是 “选一个 replica”。

Multi-model routing：

model capability：vision、tool call、long context、JSON/constrained output。 · model cost：small model first，large model fallback。 · model risk：experimental model limited traffic。 · model version：canary、shadow、rollback。

Multi-LoRA routing：

base model warm。 · adapter loaded or lazy-loaded。 · batching compatibility。 · tenant-specific adapter isolation。 · adapter cache eviction。

Multi-region routing：

user/data residency。 · latency。 · GPU availability。 · model availability。 · cross-region data transfer policy。 · regional failover。

强回答：

The router should make explicit, traceable decisions. For each request, I want to know: why this model, why this region, why this engine, why this replica, and what fallback path was allowed. That is essential for debugging, cost attribution, and customer trust.

Router decision log：

json
{
  "request_id": "req_123",
  "tenant": "enterprise_a",
  "route": "vllm-h100-us-west",
  "reason": ["model_capability", "region_affinity", "kv_available"],
  "fallback": ["vllm-a100-us-west", "smaller-model-async"],
  "policy_version": "route_policy_2026_06"
}

51. Advanced isolation：multi-tenant AI platform threat model#

Threats：

tenant A 访问 tenant B data。 · prompt/log 泄漏 PII。 · model output 包含未授权 retrieved content。 · tool call 使用过宽 credential。 · shared GPU/worker 泄漏 cache/state。 · noisy neighbor 消耗 GPU/token quota。 · regional data residency violation。

Controls：

identity：user/tenant/service identity 明确。 · authorization：retrieval ACL、tool permission、model allowlist。 · network：NetworkPolicy、private endpoint、egress control。 · compute：namespace/node pool/MIG/full GPU isolation。 · secret：scoped credentials、short-lived tokens。 · logging：redaction、sampling、no raw prompt by default。 · audit：retrieval/generation/tool/artifact events。 · quota：request/token/GPU-hour/batch backlog。

面试回答：

In AI infra, security is not only network and IAM. The model context is a data boundary. Retrieval, prompt assembly, tool execution, logs, and generated artifacts all need tenant-aware policy.

52. Design doc template：让你像 tech lead#

如果面试官问 “how would you propose this project”，可以用这个 structure：

text
Title
Problem / motivation
Non-goals
Requirements
Current architecture
Proposed architecture
API / data model
Scheduling and resource model
Failure modes
Security and isolation
Observability
Rollout plan
Capacity and cost estimate
Alternatives considered
Open questions
Milestones

Example alternatives：

vLLM vs SGLang vs TensorRT-LLM。 · Ray Serve vs KServe vs custom router。 · Kueue vs Volcano vs plain Kubernetes Job。 · full GPU vs MIG vs MPS。 · managed cloud endpoint vs self-managed K8s。

Strong phrase：

A good design doc should make tradeoffs inspectable. If we cannot explain the scheduling, rollback, security, and observability story, the design is not production-ready.

53. More coding exercises：Python / Go / SQL-ish ops#

Exercise 1: token-aware queue admission.

python
class TokenBucket:
    def __init__(self, capacity, refill_per_sec):
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.tokens = capacity
        self.refill_per_sec = refill_per_sec
        self.last = time.time()

    def allow(self, cost):
        now = time.time()
        self.tokens = min(self.capacity, self.tokens + (now - self.last) * self.refill_per_sec)
        self.last = now
        if self.tokens >= cost:
            self.tokens -= cost
            return True
        return False

面试解释：

cost 不应该是 1 request，而是 estimated input + output tokens。 · 可以按 tenant/model/workload class 做 bucket。

Exercise 2: Go controller status condition.

go
type ModelCondition struct {
    Type    string `json:"type"`
    Status  string `json:"status"`
    Reason  string `json:"reason,omitempty"`
    Message string `json:"message,omitempty"`
}

func setCondition(conds []ModelCondition, next ModelCondition) []ModelCondition {
    for i := range conds {
        if conds[i].Type == next.Type {
            conds[i] = next
            return conds
        }
    }
    return append(conds, next)
}

面试解释：

status condition 比单一 phase 更可观测。 · Ready、Warming、Degraded、RoutingActive 可以同时表达。

Exercise 3: operational query.

sql
SELECT
  model_version,
  route_reason,
  approx_percentile(ttft_ms, 0.95) AS p95_ttft,
  sum(input_tokens + output_tokens) AS total_tokens,
  count_if(fallback_reason IS NOT NULL) AS fallback_count
FROM inference_events
WHERE event_time > now() - interval '1' hour
GROUP BY model_version, route_reason
ORDER BY p95_ttft DESC;

面试解释：

线上 debug 需要能从 trace/event/warehouse 回答：哪个 model、哪个 route、哪个 tenant、哪个 version 变差。

54. KServe / Kubeflow / custom platform：Kubernetes-native ML 生态怎么定位#

面试官可能问：Why build your own platform if KServe/Kubeflow already exist?

KServe：

Kubernetes-native model serving platform，提供 InferenceService CRD。 · 封装 autoscaling、networking、health checking、server configuration。 · 支持 predictive 和 generative AI serving，也支持 canary / A-B testing。 · data plane 概念包括 Predictor、Transformer、Explainer。 · 适合统一部署多种模型 runtime，例如 vLLM、TorchServe、自定义 container。

Kubeflow Trainer：

Kubernetes-native distributed AI training platform。 · 面向 PyTorch、HuggingFace、DeepSpeed、JAX、XGBoost 等。 · legacy Training Operator 里有 PyTorchJob，更现代的 Trainer V2 更强调统一 TrainJob API。 · 适合把 distributed training/fine-tuning 变成 Kubernetes-native workload。

自研/custom platform 什么时候有必要：

需要 token-aware routing、KV-cache-aware autoscaling、multi-engine fallback。 · 需要和产品系统深度集成：tenant policy、artifact lifecycle、billing、workflow state。 · 需要跨 vLLM/SGLang/TensorRT-LLM/Triton/Ray 的统一 observability。 · 需要定制 GPU scheduling、batch/interactive colocation、multi-region failover。

强回答：

I would not rebuild KServe or Kubeflow blindly. I would use Kubernetes-native standards where they fit, and build the differentiated control plane above them: model policy, token-aware routing, workflow state, cost attribution, and product-specific safety. KServe can be a serving abstraction; Kubeflow can manage training jobs; the AI platform still owns product semantics and SLO.

55. Profiling and optimization：从 Python 到 GPU kernel 怎么定位#

官方工具定位：
